@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+Task: {{stage}}
+Frozen task inputs: {{input_manifest}}
+Authorized story data: {{context_bundle}}
+Return only the declared JSON stage result. Apply the method selected for this task.
+
+Context is a stage-authorized business projection. Historical prose is story data; current revision diagnostics are bound repair inputs.
+
+The current work's author requirements, approved style and chapter-local constraints govern creative choices. Preserve their scope and strength. A permission or suggestion is not mandatory. Diagnose conflicts explicitly rather than silently choosing a default style. Review and revision use the exact candidate's creative_baseline; retain its identity and unaffected prose. Missing evidence calls for evaluation, not invented defects or automatic prose changes.
+
+For an ordinary targeted revision with no executable prose diagnoses, return edits=[] and resolved_issue_codes=[]; preserving the exact candidate is a valid result. Aesthetic suggestions and uncertain evidence never authorize invented edits. Selected hard failures still require their diagnosed repairs.
+
+Review scores rank candidates and retain evidence; a low score or advice alone cannot invent a prose repair. The host derives the revision decision from executable work diagnoses, while the independent Quality and acceptance gates retain their own checks.
+
+Canon output schemas use exact shared local types; follow every referenced field and constraint. This representation does not change what the prose evidences or authorize planned facts as observations.

+Task: {{stage}}
+Frozen task inputs: {{input_manifest}}
+Authorized story data: {{context_bundle}}
+Return only the declared JSON stage result. Apply the method selected for this task.
+
+Context is a stage-authorized business projection. Historical prose is story data; current revision diagnostics are bound repair inputs.
+
+The current work's author requirements, approved style and chapter-local constraints govern creative choices. Preserve their scope and strength. A permission or suggestion is not mandatory. Diagnose conflicts explicitly rather than silently choosing a default style. Review and revision use the exact candidate's creative_baseline; retain its identity and unaffected prose. Missing evidence calls for evaluation, not invented defects or automatic prose changes.
+
+For an ordinary targeted revision with no executable prose diagnoses, return edits=[] and resolved_issue_codes=[]; preserving the exact candidate is a valid result. Aesthetic suggestions and uncertain evidence never authorize invented edits. Selected hard failures still require their diagnosed repairs.
+
+Review scores rank candidates and retain evidence; a low score or advice alone cannot invent a prose repair. The host derives the revision decision from executable work diagnoses, while the independent Quality and acceptance gates retain their own checks.
+
+Canon output schemas use exact shared local types; follow every referenced field and constraint. This representation does not change what the prose evidences or authorize planned facts as observations.
+
+Canon transport: when the enforced response Schema uses output_json, encode the complete canonical object as its string value. All canonical fields and checks still apply. With a directly enforced canonical Schema, return the canonical object.

+def creation_progress(database_url: str, *, project_id: str, formation_run_id: str) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
+    from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT run.current_stage,run.lifecycle,
+            (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_external_effect effect WHERE effect.project_id=run.project_id
+                AND effect.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,book_id}'=run.book_id AND effect.status='SUCCEEDED'),
+            (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_book_opening_node_receipt receipt
+                JOIN authority_book_opening_run opening USING(opening_run_id)
+                WHERE opening.project_id=run.project_id AND opening.book_id=run.book_id),
+            (SELECT COALESCE(max(accepted_through_chapter),0) FROM authority_book_production production
+                WHERE production.project_id=run.project_id AND production.book_id=run.book_id)
+            FROM authority_story_formation_run run WHERE run.formation_run_id=%s AND run.project_id=%s""",
+            [formation_run_id, project_id])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if row is None:
+        raise ValueError("CREATION_CHECKPOINT_NOT_FOUND")
+    return tuple(row)
+
+
+def ensure_creation_scope(settings: Any, args: Any, *, principal: Any, title: str, build_id: str, sha: str) -> None:
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+    from novel_forge.modules.project_authority.application import ProjectAuthorityApplication, ProjectAuthorityCommand
+    from novel_forge.modules.project_authority.query import ProjectAuthorityQuery
+    from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+
+    application = ProjectAuthorityApplication(settings.database_url, application_build_id=build_id, commit_sha=sha)
+    query = ProjectAuthorityQuery(settings.database_url)
+    try:
+        project = query.require_project(args.project_id)
+    except KernelError as error:
+        if error.code != "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND":
+            raise
+        application.execute(ProjectAuthorityCommand(definition_name="project_lifecycle.create", project_id=args.project_id,
+            workspace_id=args.workspace_id, actor_id=principal.principal_id, idempotency_key=args.project_id + ":" + args.idempotency_key + ":project",
+            trace_id="trace:" + args.idempotency_key, correlation_id=args.book_id, payload={"title": title}), principal=principal)
+        project = query.require_project(args.project_id)
+    if project["status"] != "ACTIVE":
+        raise KernelError("PROJECT_LIFECYCLE_CONFLICT", "Creation requires an active authorized project")
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(settings.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT project_id,book_id,status FROM authority_workspace WHERE workspace_id=%s", [args.workspace_id])
+        workspace = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if workspace is None:
+        application.execute(ProjectAuthorityCommand(definition_name="project_workspace.create", project_id=args.project_id,
+            workspace_id=project["workspace_id"], actor_id=principal.principal_id, idempotency_key=args.idempotency_key + ":workspace",
+            trace_id="trace:" + args.idempotency_key, correlation_id=args.book_id,
+            expected_version=project["version"], expected_current_asset_version_id=project["asset_version_id"],
+            payload={"workspace_id": args.workspace_id}), principal=principal)
+    elif workspace[0] != args.project_id or workspace[1] not in (None, "", args.book_id) or workspace[2] != "ACTIVE":
+        raise KernelError("CREATION_WORKSPACE_SCOPE_CONFLICT", args.workspace_id)

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from dataclasses import fields
+import hashlib
+
+from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.orchestrator import SingleChapterRequest
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.asset_commands import AuthorityAssetWriteCommand
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.cancellation import (
+    CancelCommand,
+    request_cancellation,
+)
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import (
+    selected_book_template,
+    verify_template_provenance,
+)
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+
+CONTRACT = "book-preprose-timeout-successor.v1"
+BINDING = "chapter_execution_recovery"
+COMMAND = "execution_replacement_chapter_command"
+
+
+def source_evidence(cursor, *, book, request, principal):
+    principal.require("book.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    principal.require("chapter.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    admission = selected_book_template(
+        cursor, project_id=book["project_id"], book_id=book["book_id"]
+    )
+    if not admission:
+        return None
+    cursor.execute(
+        "SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s AND project_id=%s FOR SHARE",
+        [request["chapter_root_operation_id"], book["project_id"]],
+    )
+    row = cursor.fetchone()
+    if not row:
+        return None
+    root = row[0]
+    if (
+        root["definition_version"] != "3.8.0"
+        or root["command_payload"]
+        .get("preparation_report", {})
+        .get("author_draft_adoption")
+        or root["actor_id"] != principal.principal_id
+        or book["state"]
+        not in {
+            "CHAPTER_SUBMITTED",
+            "CHAPTER_RUNNING",
+            "CHAPTER_WAITING_RETRY",
+            "CHAPTER_FAILED_RETRYABLE",
+        }
+        or book["active_chapter_request_id"] != request["chapter_request_id"]
+        or request["chapter_number"] != book["accepted_through_chapter"] + 1
+        or root["status"] not in {"WAITING_CHILDREN", "FAILED_RETRYABLE", "CANCELLED"}
+    ):
+        return None
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.status,e.request_hash,e.request_payload,e.last_error,r.status,o.definition_name,
+            o.definition_snapshot->'timeout_policy'->>'operation_timeout_seconds'
+        FROM authority_external_effect e JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id)
+        JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id) WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s""",
+        [root["operation_id"]],
+    )
+    effects = cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(effects) != 1:
+        return None
+    effect, status, request_hash, payload, error, settlement, name, timeout = effects[0]
+    process = (error or {}).get("production_execution", {})
+    result = process.get("execution_result", {})
+    decision = (
+        (payload.get("_production_execution") or {})
+        .get("capability_runtime", {})
+        .get("routing_decision", {})
+    )
+    source_template = verify_template_provenance(cursor, decision)
+    current = admission["policy_snapshot"]["policy"]["execution_templates"]
+    target_template = next(t for t in current if t["book_id"] == book["book_id"])
+    candidate = decision.get("selected", {}).get("candidate", {})
+    if (
+        status != "FAILED_RETRYABLE"
+        or settlement != "SETTLED"
+        or name != "chapter.memo.generate"
+        or timeout != "60"
+        or result.get("outcome") != "TIMED_OUT"
+        or process.get("process_evidence", {}).get("failure_code") != "PROCESS_TIMEOUT"
+        or result.get("output_manifest_ref")
+        or result.get("normalized_output_ref")
+        or candidate.get("provider") != "codex-cli"
+        or candidate.get("pricing", {}).get("accounting_basis") != "ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT"
+        or not source_template
+        or source_template["mode"] != "CREATIVE_VALIDATION"
+        or source_template["book_id"] != book["book_id"]
+        or candidate.get("authorization_ref")
+        != source_template["member_authorization_ref"]
+        or source_template["require_literary_qualification"]
+        or source_template["member_authorization_ref"]
+        != target_template["member_authorization_ref"]
+        or source_template["require_independent_review"]
+        != target_template["require_independent_review"]
+    ):
+        return None
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id)
+        JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id) WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND l.released_at IS NULL AND l.expires_at>NOW())""",
+        [root["operation_id"]],
+    )
+    if cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_RECOVERY_LEASE_HELD",
+            "Wait for the original execution lease",
+        )
+    cursor.execute(
+        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_book_chapter_acceptance WHERE chapter_request_id=%s)",
+        [request["chapter_request_id"]],
+    )
+    if cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+        return None
+    return {
+        "contract_version": CONTRACT,
+        "predecessor_root_operation_id": root["operation_id"],
+        "predecessor_request_id": request["chapter_request_id"],
+        "external_effect_id": effect,
+        "request_hash": request_hash,
+        "original_failure": error,
+        "policy_admission": admission,
+        "target_definition_version": "3.9.0",
+        "root": root,
+    }
+
+
+def recover_timeout(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    if not book.get("active_chapter_request_id"):
+        return False
+    old = authority.require_chapter_request(book["active_chapter_request_id"])
+    if old["command_payload"].get(COMMAND):
+        resume_successor(
+            authority, chapter_request_id=old["chapter_request_id"], principal=principal
+        )
+        return True
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        source = source_evidence(
+            uow.cursor, book=book, request=old, principal=principal
+        )
+    if not source:
+        return False
+    root = source["root"]
+    stable = (
+        "chapter-timeout:"
+        + hashlib.sha256(old["chapter_request_id"].encode()).hexdigest()
+    )
+    if root["status"] != "CANCELLED":
+        cancelled = request_cancellation(
+            authority.database_url,
+            CancelCommand(
+                operation_id=root["operation_id"],
+                project_id=book["project_id"],
+                actor_id=principal.principal_id,
+                expected_version=root["version"],
+                reason="known stopped pre-prose Memo timeout; native execution contract successor",
+                idempotency_key=stable + ":cancel",
+                trace_id=stable,
+                correlation_id=book["root_operation_id"],
+            ),
+            settle_undispatched=True,
+        )
+        if cancelled["status"] != "CANCELLED":
+            raise KernelError(
+                "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_RECOVERY_CANCELLATION_PENDING",
+                "Preserve predecessor until native cancellation completes",
+            )
+    new_id = "chapter-request:" + stable
+
+    def reserve(uow, operation_id, checkpoint):
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(b) FROM authority_book_production b WHERE book_run_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [book_run_id],
+        )
+        current = uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(r) FROM authority_book_chapter_request r WHERE chapter_request_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [old["chapter_request_id"]],
+        )
+        predecessor = uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        proof = source_evidence(
+            uow.cursor, book=current, request=predecessor, principal=principal
+        )
+        if (
+            current["version"] != book["version"]
+            or not proof
+            or proof["root"]["status"] != "CANCELLED"
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_RECOVERY_CONFLICT",
+                "Reload exact source Book and stopped execution",
+            )
+        binding = {
+            "contract_version": CONTRACT,
+            "book_run_id": book_run_id,
+            "chapter_request_id": new_id,
+            "predecessor_request_id": old["chapter_request_id"],
+            "predecessor_root_operation_id": root["operation_id"],
+            "generation": predecessor["generation"] + 1,
+        }
+        frozen = {
+            **root["command_payload"],
+            "project_id": book["project_id"],
+            "workspace_id": book["workspace_id"],
+            "book_id": book["book_id"],
+            "actor_id": principal.principal_id,
+            "idempotency_key": stable,
+            "application_build_id": authority.application_build_id,
+            "commit_sha": authority.commit_sha,
+            "trace_id": stable,
+            "correlation_id": book["root_operation_id"],
+            "preparation_report": {
+                **root["command_payload"].get("preparation_report", {}),
+                BINDING: binding,
+            },
+        }
+        payload = {
+            **predecessor["command_payload"],
+            "preparation_report": frozen["preparation_report"],
+            COMMAND: frozen,
+        }
+        uow.repositories.book_authority.create_chapter_request(
+            {
+                **predecessor,
+                "chapter_request_id": new_id,
+                "stable_idempotency_key": stable,
+                "command_payload": payload,
+            },
+            generation=binding["generation"],
+            predecessor_request_id=old["chapter_request_id"],
+        )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "UPDATE authority_child_delegation SET superseded_at=NOW(),version=version+1,updated_at=NOW() WHERE chapter_request_id=%s AND superseded_at IS NULL AND status<>'ACCEPTED'",
+            [old["chapter_request_id"]],
+        )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "UPDATE authority_book_production SET active_chapter_request_id=%s,active_child_delegation_id=NULL,state='CHAPTER_SUBMITTED',version=version+1,updated_at=NOW() WHERE book_run_id=%s AND version=%s",
+            [new_id, book_run_id, book["version"]],
+        )
+        if uow.cursor.rowcount != 1:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_RECOVERY_CONFLICT", "Book checkpoint changed"
+            )
+        proof.pop("root")
+        evidence = uow.repositories.asset_documents.materialize_output(
+            AuthorityAssetWriteCommand(
+                operation_id=operation_id,
+                project_id=book["project_id"],
+                asset_id="execution-recovery:" + operation_id,
+                asset_type="chapter_execution_recovery",
+                schema_version=CONTRACT,
+                logical_ref="execution-recoveries/" + operation_id,
+                category="execution-recovery",
+                output_key="book.execution_recovery",
+                role="completion_evidence",
+                content={
+                    **binding,
+                    "source": proof,
+                    "predecessor_request": predecessor,
+                    "provider_invoked": False,
+                    "book_acceptance": "NOT_GRANTED",
+                    "author_decision": "NOT_RUN",
+                },
+                qualification_policy=CONTRACT,
+                trace_id=stable,
+                correlation_id=book["root_operation_id"],
+            )
+        )
+        checkpoint("after_timeout_successor_reservation")
+        return {
+            "chapter_request_id": new_id,
+            "evidence_asset_version_id": evidence.asset_version_id,
+        }
+
+    authority._execute_mutation(
+        book=book,
+        action="chapter_timeout_successor",
+        event_type="book.chapter.execution_successor_reserved",
+        idempotency_key=stable + ":reserve",
+        actor_id=principal.principal_id,
+        trace_id=stable,
+        correlation_id=book["root_operation_id"],
+        causation_id=root["operation_id"],
+        payload={"predecessor_request_id": old["chapter_request_id"]},
+        handler=reserve,
+    )
+    resume_successor(authority, chapter_request_id=new_id, principal=principal)
+    return True
+
+
+def require_dispatch(database_url, *, command_payload, root_operation_id):
+    binding = (command_payload.get("preparation_report") or {}).get(BINDING)
+    if binding is None:
+        return
+    required = {
+        "contract_version",
+        "book_run_id",
+        "chapter_request_id",
+        "predecessor_request_id",
+        "predecessor_root_operation_id",
+        "generation",
+    }
+    if (
+        not isinstance(binding, dict)
+        or set(binding) != required
+        or binding.get("contract_version") != CONTRACT
+        or type(binding.get("generation")) is not int
+        or binding["generation"] < 2
+        or any(
+            not isinstance(binding[k], str) or not binding[k]
+            for k in required - {"generation"}
+        )
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_SUCCESSOR_UNLINKED",
+            "Invalid native execution successor binding",
+        )
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            """SELECT r.chapter_root_operation_id,b.active_chapter_request_id,r.generation,r.predecessor_request_id,
+            b.accepted_through_chapter,r.chapter_number,r.command_payload,d.superseded_at,d.delegation_id,
+            p.status,EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_book_chapter_acceptance a WHERE a.chapter_request_id=r.chapter_request_id)
+            FROM authority_book_chapter_request r JOIN authority_book_production b USING(book_run_id)
+            JOIN authority_child_delegation d USING(chapter_request_id)
+            JOIN authority_operation p ON p.operation_id=%s WHERE r.chapter_request_id=%s AND r.book_run_id=%s""",
+            [
+                binding["predecessor_root_operation_id"],
+                binding["chapter_request_id"],
+                binding["book_run_id"],
+            ],
+        )
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if (
+        binding.get("contract_version") != CONTRACT
+        or not row
+        or row[0] != root_operation_id
+        or row[2] != binding["generation"]
+        or row[3] != binding["predecessor_request_id"]
+        or row[6].get("preparation_report", {}).get(BINDING) != binding
+        or row[9] != "CANCELLED"
+        or row[7] is not None
+        or (
+            not row[10]
+            and (row[1] != binding["chapter_request_id"] or row[4] >= row[5])
+        )
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_SUCCESSOR_UNLINKED",
+            "Execution successor must retain its exact native Book binding",
+        )
+
+
+def resume_successor(authority, *, chapter_request_id, principal):
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(chapter_request_id)
+    principal.require("book.authority.execute", request["project_id"])
+    frozen = request["command_payload"][COMMAND]
+    if request["chapter_root_operation_id"]:
+        return request
+    book = authority.require(request["book_run_id"])
+    if book["active_chapter_request_id"] != chapter_request_id:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CHAPTER_TIMEOUT_RECOVERY_CONFLICT",
+            "Successor is not the current Book child",
+        )
+    command = {
+        f.name: frozen[f.name] for f in fields(SingleChapterRequest) if f.name in frozen
+    }
+    command["context_candidates"] = tuple(command.get("context_candidates") or ())
+    admission = authority.chapter_port.submit(
+        SingleChapterRequest(**command), principal=principal
+    )
+    current = authority.require(book["book_run_id"])

+SUPPORT_CLAIM_KINDS = ("OBJECTIVE_EVENT", "OBJECTIVE_STATE", "KNOWLEDGE_ACQUISITION", "BELIEF", "SUSPICION", "HEARSAY", "DIALOGUE_ASSERTION", "READER_DISCLOSURE", "NARRATIVE_OBSERVATION")

+def support_kind_repair_diagnostic(extraction, review):
+    claims={i['claim']['grounded_claim_id']:i['claim'] for i in grounded_claims(extraction)}
+    values=review.get('claims')
+    if (review.get('coverage')!='COMPLETE' or not isinstance(values,list) or len(values)!=len(claims)
+        or any(not isinstance(i,dict) for i in values)
+        or {i.get('claim_id') for i in values}!=set(claims)):
+        return None
+    unknown=[]
+    for item in values:
+        if item.get('assessment')!='SUPPORTED' or not isinstance(item.get('reason'),str) or not item['reason'].strip():
+            return None
+        kind=item.get('observed_kind')
+        if not isinstance(kind,str) or not kind.strip():
+            return None
+        if kind in SUPPORT_CLAIM_KINDS and kind!=claims[item['claim_id']].get('claim_kind'):
+            return None
+        if kind not in SUPPORT_CLAIM_KINDS:
+            unknown.append({'claim_id':item['claim_id'],'recorded_kind':kind})
+    if not unknown:
+        return None
+    return {'contract_version':SUPPORT_KIND_CONTRACT,'unknown_kinds':unknown,
+        'source_review_hash':text_hash(json.dumps(review,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True)),
+        'claim_set_hash':text_hash(json.dumps(grounded_claims(extraction),ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True)),
+        'assessment_reused':False}

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from .grounding import support_kind_repair_diagnostic
+
+
+def support_kind_repair_evidence(cursor, operation_id, attempt_id):
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_operation_output_asset oa
+        JOIN authority_asset x USING(asset_id) JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+        WHERE oa.operation_id=%s AND x.asset_type='model_call_log' AND v.content->>'attempt_id'=%s
+        AND v.content->>'stage'='canon_support'""", [operation_id,attempt_id])
+    rows=cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(rows)!=1:
+        raise KernelError('CANON_SUPPORT_REPAIR_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED','Bind one exact retained independent support review')
+    review=rows[0]
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_operation o,
+        jsonb_array_elements(o.input_asset_snapshot) binding
+        JOIN authority_asset_version v ON v.asset_version_id=binding->>'asset_version_id'
+            AND v.content_hash=binding->>'content_hash'
+        JOIN authority_asset x USING(asset_id)
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND x.asset_type='canon_extraction'""",[operation_id])
+    inputs=cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(inputs)!=1:
+        raise KernelError('CANON_SUPPORT_REPAIR_INPUT_REQUIRED','Retain the exact frozen Canon extraction')
+    extraction=inputs[0]
+    result=review[2].get('provider_evidence',{}).get('production_execution',{}).get('process_evidence',{}).get('result')
+    proof=support_kind_repair_diagnostic(extraction[2],result) if isinstance(result,dict) else None
+    if not proof:
+        raise KernelError('CANON_GROUNDING_REEXTRACTION_REQUIRED','Unsupported, uncertain or mismatched defined kinds require semantic repair')
+    return {**proof,'review_asset':{'asset_version_id':review[0],'content_hash':review[1]},
+        'extraction_asset':{'asset_version_id':extraction[0],'content_hash':extraction[1]}}

+def selected_validation_policy(cursor, *, project_id, root_operation_id, lock=False):
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import (
+        selected_book_template,
+        selected_template,
+    )
+
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT b.book_run_id,b.book_id,b.active_chapter_request_id,b.accepted_through_chapter,
+        b.version,r.chapter_number FROM authority_book_production b JOIN authority_book_chapter_request r
+        ON r.chapter_request_id=b.active_chapter_request_id WHERE r.chapter_root_operation_id=%s AND b.project_id=%s"""
+        + (" FOR UPDATE OF b,r" if lock else ""),
+        [root_operation_id, project_id],
+    )
+    book = cursor.fetchone()
+    admission = (
+        selected_book_template(cursor, project_id=project_id, book_id=book[1])
+        if book
+        else None
+    )
+    template = (
+        selected_template(admission["policy_snapshot"]["policy"], book[1])
+        if admission
+        else None
+    )
+    if (
+        not template
+        or template.get("version") != "creative-execution-template.v6"
+        or template.get("canon_validation_contract") != CONTRACT
+        or template.get("chapter_contract_recovery") != "KNOWN_SETTLED_CONTRACT_REPAIR"
+        or book[5] != book[3] + 1
+    ):
+        return None
+    return {
+        "book_run_id": book[0],
+        "chapter_request_id": book[2],
+        "accepted_cursor": book[3],
+        "policy_admission": admission,
+        "canon_validation_contract": CONTRACT,
+    }

+def canon_revalidation_evidence(cursor, source):
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.domain_handlers import build_canon_patch
+    from novel_forge.modules.canon_graph.approval_domain import resolve_canon_extraction
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.cost_routing import snapshot_hash
+
+    inputs = {}
+    decisions = []
+    for binding in source["input_asset_snapshot"]:
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT a.asset_type,v.content,v.content_hash FROM authority_asset_version v JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) WHERE v.asset_version_id=%s",
+            [binding["asset_version_id"]],
+        )
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or row[2] != binding["content_hash"]:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "CANON_REVALIDATION_INPUT_DRIFT", "Retain the exact frozen Canon inputs"
+            )
+        if row[0] == "canon_approval_decision":
+            decisions.append(
+                {**row[1], "asset_version_id": binding["asset_version_id"]}
+            )
+        else:
+            inputs[row[0]] = (binding["asset_version_id"], row[1])
+    cursor.execute(
+        "SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_operation_output_asset o JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.role='primary'",
+        [source["operation_id"]],
+    )

+    required = {
+        "best_draft_candidate",
+        "canon_extraction",
+        "canon_change_proposal",
+        "context_bundle",
+    }
+    if not old or not required.issubset(inputs):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CANON_REVALIDATION_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
+            "Retain the completed pure Canon reduction and its grounded inputs",
+        )
+    resolved = resolve_canon_extraction(
+        extraction=inputs["canon_extraction"][1],
+        proposal={

+            "asset_version_id": inputs["canon_change_proposal"][0],
+        },
+        decisions=decisions,
+    )
+    args = dict(
+        project_id=source["project_id"],
+        revision=inputs["best_draft_candidate"][1],
+        revision_asset_version_id=inputs["best_draft_candidate"][0],
+        context_bundle_version_id=inputs["context_bundle"][0],
+        context_bundle=inputs["context_bundle"][1],
+        extraction=resolved,
+    )
+    legacy = build_canon_patch(**args)
+    current = build_canon_patch(**args, validation_contract=CONTRACT)
+    changed = sorted(
+        k for k in set(legacy) | set(current) if legacy.get(k) != current.get(k)
+    )
+    if (
+        legacy != old[2]
+        or not legacy["conflicts"]
+        or legacy["gate"] != "BLOCKED"
+        or not changed
+        or not set(changed).issubset({"conflicts", "deterministic_validation", "gate"})
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "CANON_REVALIDATION_NOT_APPLICABLE",
+            "Only a reproduced derived validator diagnostic may receive this successor",
+        )
+    return {
+        "contract_version": "chapter-canon-derived-revalidation.v1",
+        "source_output": {"asset_version_id": old[0], "content_hash": old[1]},
+        "input_snapshot": source["input_asset_snapshot"],
+        "legacy_reconstructed_exactly": True,
+        "changed_fields": changed,
+        "corrected_content_hash": snapshot_hash(current),
+        "facts_and_state_changed": False,
+        "provider_invoked": False,
+    }

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.cancellation import CancelCommand, request_cancellation
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.service import AdmissionContext
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.step_successor import replace_failed_step, has_candidate_domain_outputs
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import selected_book_template
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork

+def recover_information_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    principal.require('book.authority.execute', book['project_id'])
+    principal.require('chapter.authority.execute', book['project_id'])
+    if not book.get('active_chapter_request_id'):
+        return False
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(book['active_chapter_request_id'])
+    if request['chapter_number'] != book['accepted_through_chapter'] + 1:
+        return False
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        template = selected_book_template(uow.cursor, project_id=book['project_id'], book_id=book['book_id'])
+        if not template:
+            return False
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT to_jsonb(o),to_jsonb(a) FROM authority_operation o
+            JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id) JOIN authority_attempt a USING(operation_id)
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.definition_name='chapter.draft.generate'
+            AND o.definition_version='3.9.1' AND o.status IN ('FAILED_RETRYABLE','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED')
+            AND a.result->>'error_code'='REALIZATION_EPISTEMIC_SCOPE_VIOLATION' ORDER BY a.attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1""", [request['chapter_root_operation_id'], book['project_id']])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            return False
+        source, attempt = row
+        if source['actor_id'] != principal.principal_id:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_CANDIDATE_RECOVERY_ACTOR_INVALID', 'Recovery requires the original actor')
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_hash,e.status,r.status FROM authority_external_effect e
+            LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id) WHERE e.operation_id=%s ORDER BY e.external_effect_id""", [source['operation_id']])
+        effects = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if not effects or any(r[2:] != ('SUCCEEDED','SETTLED') for r in effects):
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_CANDIDATE_RECOVERY_UNSETTLED', 'Reconcile original effects before candidate recovery')
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_operation_output_asset WHERE operation_id=%s),
+            EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_operation_dependency WHERE depends_on_operation_id=%s),
+            EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id)
+                WHERE a.operation_id=%s AND l.released_at IS NULL AND l.expires_at>NOW())""", [source['operation_id']]*3)
+        _, dependent, lease = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if dependent or lease or has_candidate_domain_outputs(uow.cursor, source['operation_id']):
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_CANDIDATE_RECOVERY_STARTED', 'Outputs, dependents or live leases prevent succession')
+    evidence = {'contract_version':'chapter-candidate-contract-repair.v1', 'book_run_id':book_run_id,
+        'chapter_request_id':request['chapter_request_id'], 'policy_admission':template,
+        'input_snapshot':source['input_asset_snapshot'], 'source_failure':attempt,
+        'source_operation_before_cancellation':source, 'settled_candidate_effects':effects,
+        'completion_checks':'UNCHANGED', 'accepted_cursor':book['accepted_through_chapter']}
+    if source['status'] != 'CANCELLATION_REQUESTED':
+        request_cancellation(authority.database_url, CancelCommand(operation_id=source['operation_id'],
+            project_id=book['project_id'],actor_id=principal.principal_id,reason='Known settled candidate requires versioned information-flow repair',
+            expected_version=source['version'],idempotency_key='candidate-repair:'+source['operation_id'],
+            trace_id=source['trace_id'],correlation_id=source['correlation_id']))
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.execution_window import continue_candidate_execution_window
+    continue_candidate_execution_window(authority.database_url, root_id=request['chapter_root_operation_id'],
+        predecessor_id=source['operation_id'],target_version='3.9.2',book_run_id=book_run_id,principal=principal)
+    def guard(uow):
+        uow.cursor.execute('SELECT version,active_chapter_request_id,accepted_through_chapter FROM authority_book_production WHERE book_run_id=%s AND project_id=%s FOR UPDATE', [book_run_id,book['project_id']])
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone() != (book['version'],book['active_chapter_request_id'],book['accepted_through_chapter']) or selected_book_template(uow.cursor,project_id=book['project_id'],book_id=book['book_id']) != template:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_CANDIDATE_RECOVERY_CONFLICT', 'Reload the exact current Book frontier')
+    replace_failed_step(authority.database_url,predecessor_id=source['operation_id'],target_version='3.9.2',
+        context=AdmissionContext(principal.principal_id,principal.permissions,authority.chapter_port.application.registry.available_handler_keys,
+            authority.application_build_id,authority.commit_sha), policy_evidence=evidence,
+        expected_failure_code='REALIZATION_EPISTEMIC_SCOPE_VIOLATION',
+        settled_candidate_effects=tuple((r[0],r[1]) for r in effects),transaction_guard=guard)
+    return True

+def candidate_repair_payload(request):
+    if request.definition_version not in {'3.9.2','3.9.3','3.9.4'} or not request.database_url:
+        return {}
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(request.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute('''SELECT e.external_effect_id,a.attempt_id,a.result,r.payload->'evidence'
+            FROM authority_attempt a JOIN authority_external_effect e ON e.operation_id=a.operation_id
+                AND e.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,attempt_id}'=a.attempt_id
+            JOIN authority_budget_reservation b USING(external_effect_id)
+            JOIN authority_external_effect_dispatch d USING(external_effect_id)
+            JOIN authority_external_receipt r USING(dispatch_id)
+            WHERE a.operation_id=%s AND e.project_id=%s AND a.attempt_id<>%s
+                AND a.status='FAILED_RETRYABLE' AND (a.result->>'error_code' LIKE 'REALIZATION_%%' OR a.result->>'error_code'='CHAPTER_LENGTH_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE')
+                AND e.status='SUCCEEDED' AND b.status='SETTLED'
+            ORDER BY a.attempt_number DESC,r.received_at DESC LIMIT 1''',
+            [request.operation_id,request.project_id,request.attempt_id])
+        recent = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if recent:
+            from .model_effects import ModelProviderResult
+            result = ModelProviderResult.from_evidence(recent[3])
+            measured = {}
+            if request.definition_version == '3.9.4':
+                from .realization_composition import prose_from_output
+                from novel_forge.modules.revision.domain import han_characters
+                measured = {'length_observation':{'unit':'HAN_CHARACTERS_V1','observed':han_characters(prose_from_output(result.output))}}
+            return {'candidate_repair': {'contract_version':'chapter-candidate-output-repair.v1',
+                'source_effect_id':recent[0], 'source_attempt_id':recent[1], 'diagnostic':recent[2],
+                'rejected_candidate':result.output, **measured,
+                'instruction':'Repair this latest rejected candidate under the exact current slots and original work requirements. No invented facts, future knowledge, padding or fabricated approval.'}}
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT policy_evidence FROM authority_saga_step_successor
+            WHERE project_id=%s AND successor_operation_id=%s""", [request.project_id,request.operation_id])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or row[0].get('contract_version') not in {'chapter-candidate-contract-repair.v1','chapter-review-output-repair.v1','chapter-length-output-repair.v1'}:
+            return {}
+        binding = row[0]
+        effect_id = binding['settled_candidate_effects'][-1][0]
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT r.payload->'evidence' FROM authority_external_receipt r
+            JOIN authority_external_effect_dispatch d USING(dispatch_id) JOIN authority_external_effect e USING(external_effect_id)
+            JOIN authority_budget_reservation b USING(external_effect_id)
+            WHERE e.external_effect_id=%s AND e.project_id=%s AND e.status='SUCCEEDED' AND b.status='SETTLED'
+            ORDER BY r.received_at DESC LIMIT 1""", [effect_id,request.project_id])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_CANDIDATE_REPAIR_EVIDENCE_MISSING', 'Retained candidate requires its exact succeeded receipt')
+    from .model_effects import ModelProviderResult
+    result = ModelProviderResult.from_evidence(row[0])
+    return {'candidate_repair': {'contract_version':binding['contract_version'],
+        'source_effect_id':effect_id,'source_attempt_id':binding['source_failure']['attempt_id'],
+        'diagnostic':binding['source_failure']['result'], 'rejected_candidate':result.output,
+        'instruction':('Repair the actual review against the exact delivered knowledge_review_targets; an empty target list requires knowledge_checks=[]. Retain valid evidenced prose diagnoses; do not fabricate verification, approval or resolution.' if binding['contract_version']=='chapter-review-output-repair.v1' else 'Repair the actual retained revision under the exact chapter_length_repair_scope and supplied review; preserve scene coverage, facts, information boundaries and voice. Never claim acceptance or literary proof.' if binding['contract_version']=='chapter-length-output-repair.v1' else 'Repair the actual candidate using the new exact slot scopes. Meet the original work length without repetition or padding. Do not invent fact ids, future knowledge, approval or acceptance.')}}
+

+def _recover_settled_output_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal, definition_name, target_version, contract_version, failure_codes, statuses):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    principal.require('book.authority.execute', book['project_id'])
+    principal.require('chapter.authority.execute', book['project_id'])
+    if not book.get('active_chapter_request_id'):
+        return False
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(book['active_chapter_request_id'])
+    if request['chapter_number'] != book['accepted_through_chapter'] + 1:
+        return False
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import selected_template
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        template = selected_book_template(uow.cursor, project_id=book['project_id'], book_id=book['book_id'])
+        policy = selected_template(template['policy_snapshot']['policy'], book['book_id']) if template else None
+        if not policy or policy.get('chapter_contract_recovery') != 'KNOWN_SETTLED_CONTRACT_REPAIR':
+            return False
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT to_jsonb(o),to_jsonb(a) FROM authority_operation o
+            JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id)
+            JOIN LATERAL (SELECT * FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=o.operation_id ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1) a ON TRUE
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.definition_name=%s
+            AND o.definition_version='3.9.0' AND o.status=ANY(%s)
+            AND a.result->>'error_code'=ANY(%s)
+            ORDER BY a.attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1""", [request['chapter_root_operation_id'], book['project_id'],definition_name,list(statuses),list(failure_codes)])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            return False
+        source, attempt = row
+        if source['actor_id'] != principal.principal_id:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_REVIEW_RECOVERY_ACTOR_INVALID', 'Recovery requires the original review actor')
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_hash,e.status,r.status FROM authority_external_effect e
+            LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id) WHERE e.operation_id=%s ORDER BY e.external_effect_id""", [source['operation_id']])
+        effects = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if not effects or any(r[2:] != ('SUCCEEDED','SETTLED') for r in effects):
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_REVIEW_RECOVERY_UNSETTLED', 'Reconcile original review effects before recovery')
+    support_proof = None
+    if contract_version == 'chapter-support-contract-repair.v1':
+        from novel_forge.modules.canon_graph.support_recovery import support_kind_repair_evidence
+        with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+            support_proof = support_kind_repair_evidence(uow.cursor, source['operation_id'], attempt['attempt_id'])
+    quality_proof = None
+    if contract_version == 'chapter-quality-contract-repair.v1':
+        from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.applicability_contract import quality_applicability_repair_evidence
+        with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+            quality_proof=quality_applicability_repair_evidence(uow.cursor,source['operation_id'],attempt['attempt_id'])
+    length_scope = None
+    if contract_version == 'chapter-length-output-repair.v1':
+        from novel_forge.storage.authority_asset_store import AuthorityAssetQuery
+        from novel_forge.modules.revision.domain import freeze_chapter_length_scope
+        query = AuthorityAssetQuery(authority.database_url)
+        inputs = [query.require(project_id=book['project_id'],asset_version_id=a['asset_version_id']) for a in source['input_asset_snapshot']]
+        if any(asset.content_hash != binding['content_hash'] for asset,binding in zip(inputs,source['input_asset_snapshot'],strict=True)):
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_RECOVERY_INPUT_DRIFT', 'Length recovery requires its exact frozen inputs')
+        draft = next(asset for asset in inputs if asset.asset_type=='draft_chapter')
+        context = next(asset for asset in inputs if asset.asset_type=='context_bundle')
+        length_scope = freeze_chapter_length_scope(draft.content,source_asset_version_id=draft.asset_version_id,
+            context=context.content,context_asset_version_id=context.asset_version_id)
+        if length_scope is None:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_RECOVERY_SCOPE_REQUIRED', 'A local edit violation without measured length deficiency retains its original recovery path')
+    evidence = {'contract_version':contract_version, 'book_run_id':book_run_id,
+        'chapter_request_id':request['chapter_request_id'], 'policy_admission':template,
+        'input_snapshot':source['input_asset_snapshot'], 'source_failure':attempt,
+        'source_operation':source, 'settled_candidate_effects':effects,
+        **({'length_scope':length_scope} if length_scope is not None else {}),
+        **({'support_kind_repair':support_proof} if support_proof is not None else {}),
+        **({'quality_applicability_repair':quality_proof} if quality_proof is not None else {}),
+        'completion_checks':'UNCHANGED', 'accepted_cursor':book['accepted_through_chapter']}
+    def guard(uow):
+        uow.cursor.execute('SELECT version,active_chapter_request_id,accepted_through_chapter FROM authority_book_production WHERE book_run_id=%s AND project_id=%s FOR UPDATE', [book_run_id,book['project_id']])
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone() != (book['version'],book['active_chapter_request_id'],book['accepted_through_chapter']) or selected_book_template(uow.cursor,project_id=book['project_id'],book_id=book['book_id']) != template:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_REVIEW_RECOVERY_CONFLICT', 'Reload the exact current Book frontier')
+    if source['status'] not in {'FAILED_FINAL','DEAD_LETTER','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED'}:
+        request_cancellation(authority.database_url, CancelCommand(operation_id=source['operation_id'], project_id=book['project_id'],
+            actor_id=principal.principal_id,reason='Known settled output requires its explicit versioned correction contract',
+            expected_version=source['version'],idempotency_key='output-repair:'+source['operation_id'],
+            trace_id=source['trace_id'],correlation_id=source['correlation_id']))
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.execution_window import continue_candidate_execution_window
+    continue_candidate_execution_window(authority.database_url, root_id=request['chapter_root_operation_id'],
+        predecessor_id=source['operation_id'],target_version=target_version,book_run_id=book_run_id,principal=principal)
+    replace_failed_step(authority.database_url,predecessor_id=source['operation_id'],target_version=target_version,
+        context=AdmissionContext(principal.principal_id,principal.permissions,authority.chapter_port.application.registry.available_handler_keys,
+            authority.application_build_id,authority.commit_sha), policy_evidence=evidence,
+        expected_failure_code=attempt['result']['error_code'],
+        settled_candidate_effects=tuple((r[0],r[1]) for r in effects),transaction_guard=guard)
+    return True
+
+
+def recover_review_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    return _recover_settled_output_frontier(authority, book_run_id=book_run_id, principal=principal,
+        definition_name='chapter.review.evaluate', target_version='3.9.3', contract_version='chapter-review-output-repair.v1',
+        failure_codes=('REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_INVALID','REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_REQUIRED'), statuses=('FAILED_FINAL',))
+
+
+def recover_support_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    return _recover_settled_output_frontier(authority, book_run_id=book_run_id, principal=principal,
+        definition_name='chapter.canon.propose_changes', target_version='3.9.8', contract_version='chapter-support-contract-repair.v1',
+        failure_codes=('CANON_GROUNDING_REEXTRACTION_REQUIRED',), statuses=('FAILED_RETRYABLE','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED'))
+
+
+def recover_length_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    return _recover_settled_output_frontier(authority, book_run_id=book_run_id, principal=principal,
+        definition_name='chapter.revision.apply', target_version='3.9.4', contract_version='chapter-length-output-repair.v1',
+        failure_codes=('REVISION_OUTSIDE_DIAGNOSIS_SCOPE',), statuses=('FAILED_RETRYABLE','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED'))
+
+
+def recover_length_retry_phase(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    principal.require('book.authority.execute',book['project_id'])
+    principal.require('chapter.authority.execute',book['project_id'])
+    if not book.get('active_chapter_request_id'):
+        return False
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(book['active_chapter_request_id'])
+    if request['chapter_number'] != book['accepted_through_chapter']+1:
+        return False
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import selected_template
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        admission = selected_book_template(uow.cursor, project_id=book['project_id'], book_id=book['book_id'])
+        template = selected_template(admission['policy_snapshot']['policy'],book['book_id']) if admission else None
+        if not template or template.get('chapter_contract_recovery') != 'KNOWN_SETTLED_CONTRACT_REPAIR':
+            return False
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT o.operation_id,o.actor_id,a.attempt_number,(o.definition_snapshot #>> '{retry_policy,max_attempts}')::int,a.result->>'error_code'
+            FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id)
+            JOIN LATERAL (SELECT * FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=o.operation_id ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1) a ON TRUE
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.definition_name='chapter.revision.apply'
+            AND o.definition_version='3.9.4' AND o.status='FAILED_RETRYABLE'
+            AND a.result->>'error_code' IN ('CHAPTER_LENGTH_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE','OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT')
+            ORDER BY a.attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1""",[request['chapter_root_operation_id'],book['project_id']])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or row[2]>row[3] or (row[2]==row[3] and row[4]!='OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT'):
+            return False
+        if row[4] == 'OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT':
+            uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=%s AND attempt_number=%s AND result->>'error_code'='CHAPTER_LENGTH_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE'),
+                EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect e JOIN authority_attempt a ON a.operation_id=e.operation_id AND a.attempt_number=%s
+                    WHERE e.operation_id=%s AND e.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,attempt_id}'=a.attempt_id),
+                (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=%s AND result->>'error_code'='OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT')""",[row[0],row[2]-1,row[2],row[0],row[0]])
+            known_length,sent,timeouts = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+            if not known_length or sent or timeouts != 1:
+                return False
+        if row[1] != principal.principal_id:
+            raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_RETRY_ACTOR_INVALID','Retry requires the original actor')

+        predecessor_id=row[0],target_version='3.9.4',book_run_id=book_run_id,principal=principal,start_repair_phase=True)
+    return True

+                    try:
+                        canon_revalidation_evidence(uow.cursor,row[0])
+                        return False

+                            raise

+        changed=_recover_settled_output_frontier(authority,book_run_id=book_run_id,principal=principal,

+            failure_codes=('QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_CONFLICT',),statuses=('DEAD_LETTER',)) or changed
+    return changed

+def recover_realization_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    principal.require("book.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    principal.require("chapter.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    if not book.get("active_chapter_request_id"):
+        return False
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(book["active_chapter_request_id"])
+    if request["chapter_number"] != book["accepted_through_chapter"] + 1:
+        return False
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        template = selected_book_template(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=book["project_id"], book_id=book["book_id"]
+        )
+        if not template:
+            return False
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            """SELECT o.operation_id,o.command_payload,o.input_asset_snapshot FROM authority_operation o
+            JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id) JOIN authority_attempt a USING(operation_id)
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.definition_name='chapter.draft.generate'
+              AND o.definition_version='3.9.0' AND o.status='FAILED_FINAL'
+              AND a.result->>'error_code'='REALIZATION_CAPABILITY_INVALID'""",
+            [request["chapter_root_operation_id"], book["project_id"]],
+        )
+        source = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not source:
+        return False
+    query = AuthorityAssetQuery(authority.database_url)
+    inputs = [
+        query.require(
+            project_id=book["project_id"], asset_version_id=a["asset_version_id"]
+        )
+        for a in source[2]
+    ]
+    plan = next(a.content for a in inputs if a.asset_type == "chapter_plan")
+    context = next(a.content for a in inputs if a.asset_type == "context_bundle")
+    compiled = compile_realization_contract(
+        plan,
+        build_draft_style_view(context, work_requirements=True),
+        core_policy="scene-core-slots.v1",
+    )
+    evidence = {
+        "contract_version": "chapter-core-contract-recovery.v1",
+        "book_run_id": book_run_id,
+        "chapter_request_id": request["chapter_request_id"],
+        "policy_admission": template,
+        "input_snapshot": source[2],
+        "core_projection": compiled["core_projection"],
+        "completion_checks": "UNCHANGED",
+        "accepted_cursor": book["accepted_through_chapter"],
+    }
+
+    def guard(uow):
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT version,active_chapter_request_id,accepted_through_chapter FROM authority_book_production WHERE book_run_id=%s AND project_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [book_run_id, book["project_id"]],
+        )
+        current = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        fresh_template = selected_book_template(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=book["project_id"], book_id=book["book_id"]
+        )
+        if (
+            current
+            != (
+                book["version"],
+                book["active_chapter_request_id"],
+                book["accepted_through_chapter"],
+            )
+            or fresh_template != template
+        ):
+            from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+
+            raise KernelError(
+                "CHAPTER_CONTRACT_RECOVERY_CONFLICT",
+                "Reload the exact Book frontier and policy",
+            )
+
+    replace_failed_step(
+        authority.database_url,
+        predecessor_id=source[0],
+        target_version="3.9.2",
+        context=AdmissionContext(
+            principal.principal_id,
+            principal.permissions,
+            authority.chapter_port.application.registry.available_handler_keys,
+            authority.application_build_id,
+            authority.commit_sha,
+        ),
+        policy_evidence=evidence,
+        expected_failure_code="REALIZATION_CAPABILITY_INVALID",
+        transaction_guard=guard,
+    )
+    return True

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from novel_forge.modules.canon_graph.validation_recovery import (
+    selected_validation_policy,
+    canon_revalidation_evidence,
+)
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.derived_step_revalidation import (
+    replace_derived_step,
+    CONTRACT,
+)
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.service import AdmissionContext
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+
+
+def recover_derived_frontier(authority, *, book_run_id, principal):
+    book = authority.require(book_run_id)
+    principal.require("book.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    principal.require("chapter.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    if not book.get("active_chapter_request_id"):
+        return False
+    request = authority.require_chapter_request(book["active_chapter_request_id"])
+    root_id = request["chapter_root_operation_id"]
+    project = book["project_id"]
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(authority.database_url) as uow:
+        c = uow.cursor
+        admission = selected_validation_policy(
+            c, project_id=project, root_operation_id=root_id
+        )
+        if not admission or admission["book_run_id"] != book_run_id:
+            return False
+        from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.revalidation import quality_revalidation_evidence
+        c.execute("""SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id)
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND ((o.definition_name='chapter.quality.longform.evaluate' AND o.definition_version='3.9.9' AND o.status='SUCCEEDED')
+              OR (o.definition_name='chapter.quality.evaluate' AND o.definition_version='3.9.0' AND o.status='SUCCEEDED')
+              OR (o.definition_name='chapter.publishing.prepare_release' AND o.definition_version='3.9.0'))
+            ORDER BY CASE o.definition_name WHEN 'chapter.quality.longform.evaluate' THEN 1 WHEN 'chapter.quality.evaluate' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END""", [root_id])
+        quality_source = None
+        for (candidate,) in c.fetchall():
+            try:
+                correction = quality_revalidation_evidence(c,candidate)
+            except KernelError as error:
+                if error.code in {'QUALITY_REVALIDATION_NOT_APPLICABLE','QUALITY_REVALIDATION_NOT_READY'}:
+                    continue
+                raise
+            quality_source = candidate
+            quality_evidence = {'admission':admission,'quality_revalidation':correction}
+            break
+        if quality_source:
+            source = quality_source
+            evidence = quality_evidence
+        c.execute(
+            """SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id)
+            WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.definition_name='chapter.canon.update' """,
+            [root_id],
+        )
+        row = c.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            return False
+        canon = row[0]
+        source = quality_source
+        evidence = quality_evidence if quality_source else None
+        if not source and canon["definition_version"] == "3.9.0" and canon["status"] == "SUCCEEDED":
+            try:
+                diagnostic = canon_revalidation_evidence(c, canon)
+            except KernelError as error:
+                if error.code == "CANON_REVALIDATION_NOT_APPLICABLE":
+                    return False
+                raise
+            source = canon
+            evidence = {"admission": admission, "diagnostic": diagnostic}
+        elif not source and canon["definition_version"] == "3.9.10" and canon["status"] == "SUCCEEDED":
+            c.execute(
+                "SELECT predecessor_operation_id,policy_evidence FROM authority_saga_step_revalidation WHERE successor_operation_id=%s AND contract_version=%s",
+                [canon["operation_id"], CONTRACT],
+            )
+            lineage = c.fetchone()
+            if not lineage:
+                return False
+            c.execute(
+                "SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash FROM authority_operation_output_asset o JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.role='primary'",
+                [canon["operation_id"]],
+            )
+            current = c.fetchone()
+            canonical_replacement = {
+                "predecessor_operation_id": lineage[0],
+                "successor_operation_id": canon["operation_id"],
+                "old_input": lineage[1]["diagnostic"]["source_output"],
+                "new_input": {
+                    "asset_version_id": current[0],
+                    "content_hash": current[1],
+                },
+            }
+            c.execute(
+                """SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id)
+                WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND o.definition_version='3.9.0'
+                  AND ((o.definition_name='chapter.quality.longform.evaluate' AND o.status='SUCCEEDED')
+                    OR (o.definition_name='chapter.quality.commercial.evaluate' AND o.status='DEAD_LETTER'))
+                ORDER BY o.definition_name LIMIT 1""",
+                [root_id],
+            )
+            row = c.fetchone()
+            if not row:
+                return False
+            source = row[0]
+            evidence = {
+                "admission": admission,
+                "canonical_input_replacement": canonical_replacement,
+            }
+            if source["status"] == "DEAD_LETTER":
+                from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.applicability_contract import (
+                    quality_applicability_repair_evidence,
+                )
+
+                c.execute(
+                    "SELECT attempt_id FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=%s ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1",
+                    [source["operation_id"]],
+                )
+                evidence["diagnostic"] = quality_applicability_repair_evidence(
+                    c, source["operation_id"], c.fetchone()[0]
+                )
+        if source is None:
+            return False
+    replace_derived_step(
+        authority.database_url,
+        predecessor_id=source["operation_id"],
+        context=AdmissionContext(
+            principal.principal_id,
+            principal.permissions,
+            authority.chapter_port.application.registry.available_handler_keys,
+            authority.application_build_id,
+            authority.commit_sha,
+        ),
+        policy_evidence=evidence,
+    )
+    return True

 _SCHEMA_DIALECT = "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
+
+
+def shared_canon_schema(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
+    def identity(value):
+        return json.dumps(value,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False)
+    def collect(value):
+        if isinstance(value,dict):
+            if any(k in value for k in ('$id','$anchor','$ref','$defs')):
+                raise KernelError('CANON_SHARED_SCHEMA_SCOPE_INVALID','Only a complete inline Schema can receive shared local types')
+            if len(identity(value))>=96 and (isinstance(value.get('type'),(str,list)) or any(isinstance(value.get(k),list) for k in ('anyOf','oneOf','allOf'))):
+                key=identity(value)
+                counts[key]=counts.get(key,0)+1
+            for item in value.values():
+                collect(item)
+        elif isinstance(value,list):
+            for item in value:
+                collect(item)
+    collect(schema)
+    names = {key:"s"+str(i) for i,key in enumerate(sorted(k for k,n in counts.items() if n>1))}
+    definitions = {}
+    def project(value, *, inline=False):
+        if isinstance(value,dict):
+            key=identity(value)
+            if not inline and counts.get(key,0)>1:
+                name=names[key]
+                if name not in definitions:
+                    definitions[name]=project(value,inline=True)
+                return {'$ref':'#/$defs/'+name}
+            return {k:project(v) for k,v in value.items()}
+        if isinstance(value,list):
+            return [project(v) for v in value]
+        return value
+    result=project(schema,inline=True)
+    if definitions:
+        result['$defs']=definitions
+    return result
+
+
+def inline_shared_canon_schema(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    definitions = schema.get('$defs')
+    if not isinstance(definitions,dict):
+        return dict(schema)
+    def expand(value, active=()):
+        if isinstance(value,dict):
+            if '$ref' in value:
+                reference=value['$ref']
+                if set(value)!={'$ref'} or not isinstance(reference,str) or not reference.startswith('#/$defs/'):
+                    raise ValueError('only exact local shared Schema references are permitted')
+                name=reference[len('#/$defs/'):]
+                if name not in definitions or name in active:
+                    raise ValueError('missing or recursive shared Schema definition')
+                return expand(definitions[name],(*active,name))
+            return {k:expand(v,active) for k,v in value.items() if k!='$defs'}
+        if isinstance(value,list):
+            return [expand(v,active) for v in value]
+        return value
+    return expand(dict(schema))

+def bind_delivered_knowledge_scope(schema: dict[str, Any], targets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    from copy import deepcopy
+    result = deepcopy(schema)
+    identities = [target['knowledge_card_asset_version_id'] for target in targets]
+    if any(not isinstance(identity, str) or not identity for identity in identities) or len(set(identities)) != len(identities):
+        raise KernelError('REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_TARGET_INVALID', 'Scope requires unique exact delivered Card versions')
+    checks = result['properties']['knowledge_checks']
+    checks.update(minItems=len(identities), maxItems=len(identities))
+    if identities:
+        checks['items']['properties']['knowledge_card_asset_version_id'] = {'type':'string','enum':sorted(identities)}
+    return result

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from copy import deepcopy
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from .domain import QUALITY_GROUPS
+
+CONTRACT = "quality-host-applicability.v1"
+
+
+def allowed_quality_statuses(schema, baseline, group):
+    requirements=baseline.get('method_requirements',{})
+    general=list(schema['properties']['dimensions']['items']['properties']['gate_status']['enum'])
+    return {dimension: ['NOT_APPLICABLE'] if requirements.get(dimension,{}).get('status') in {'NOT_APPLICABLE','PROHIBITED'}
+        else ['INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE'] if requirements.get(dimension,{}).get('status')=='UNDETERMINED'
+        else [value for value in general if value!='NOT_APPLICABLE'] for dimension in QUALITY_GROUPS[group]}
+
+
+def bind_quality_applicability_schema(schema, baseline, group):
+    result=deepcopy(schema)
+    statuses=allowed_quality_statuses(schema,baseline,group)
+    branches=[]
+    for dimension,allowed in statuses.items():
+        item=deepcopy(schema['properties']['dimensions']['items'])
+        item['properties']['dimension']={'type':'string','enum':[dimension]}
+        item['properties']['gate_status']={'type':'string','enum':allowed}
+        branches.append(item)
+    result['properties']['dimensions']['items']={'anyOf':branches}
+    result['properties']['dimensions']['minItems']=len(branches)
+    result['properties']['dimensions']['maxItems']=len(branches)
+    return result
+
+
+def quality_applicability_repair_evidence(cursor,operation_id,attempt_id):
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content,e.request_payload->'input' FROM authority_operation_output_asset oa
+        JOIN authority_asset x USING(asset_id) JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+        JOIN authority_external_effect e ON e.external_effect_id=v.content->>'external_effect_id'
+        WHERE oa.operation_id=%s AND x.asset_type='model_call_log' AND v.content->>'attempt_id'=%s""",[operation_id,attempt_id])
+    rows=cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(rows)!=1:
+        raise KernelError('QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_REPAIR_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED','Retain one exact settled Quality review')
+    asset_id,asset_hash,log,payload=rows[0]
+    output=log.get('provider_evidence',{}).get('production_execution',{}).get('process_evidence',{}).get('result',{})
+    group=str(log.get('stage') or '').removeprefix('quality_')
+    baseline=payload.get('quality_context',{}).get('creative_baseline',{})
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content->'creative_baseline' FROM authority_operation o,
+        jsonb_array_elements(o.input_asset_snapshot) binding JOIN authority_asset_version v ON v.asset_version_id=binding->>'asset_version_id'
+        AND v.content_hash=binding->>'content_hash' JOIN authority_asset x USING(asset_id)
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND x.asset_type='best_draft_candidate'""",[operation_id])
+    inputs=cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(inputs)!=1 or inputs[0][2]!=baseline or not baseline or group not in QUALITY_GROUPS:
+        raise KernelError('QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_REPAIR_INPUT_REQUIRED','Review requires its exact frozen candidate baseline')
+    dimensions=output.get('dimensions',[])
+    if (len(dimensions)!=len(QUALITY_GROUPS[group]) or {d.get('dimension') for d in dimensions}!=set(QUALITY_GROUPS[group])):
+        raise KernelError('QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_REPAIR_COVERAGE_REQUIRED','Retain complete Quality dimension coverage')
+    requirements=baseline.get('method_requirements',{})
+    invalid=[d['dimension'] for d in dimensions if d.get('gate_status')=='NOT_APPLICABLE'
+        and requirements.get(d['dimension'],{}).get('status','APPLICABLE') not in {'NOT_APPLICABLE','PROHIBITED'}]
+    if not invalid:
+        raise KernelError('QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_REPAIR_DIAGNOSTIC_REQUIRED','Only a recorded forbidden non-applicability may adopt this contract')
+    return {'contract_version':CONTRACT,'source_review_asset':{'asset_version_id':asset_id,'content_hash':asset_hash},
+        'candidate_asset':{'asset_version_id':inputs[0][0],'content_hash':inputs[0][1]},
+        'baseline_digest':baseline['baseline_digest'],'invalid_non_applicability':invalid,'assessment_reused':False}

+def internal_required_actions(*, admission, evidence_reports, target):
+    actions = []

+            'issue': {'code': f'QUALITY_{dimension.upper()}_FAILED', 'severity': 'BLOCKING', 'status': 'OPEN'},
+            'required_action': action, 'permitted_change_scope': 'CHAPTER' if located_failure else 'EVIDENCE_ONLY',
+            'required_authority': 'CHAPTER_AUTHORITY' if located_failure else 'QUALITY_AUTHORITY',
+            'preserved_invariants': ['accepted_canon', 'executed_chapter_history', 'active_parent_plan_identity'],
+            'reevaluation_gate': report.get('gate_type', ''), 'disposition_contract': 'creative-quality-disposition.v2',
+            'strict_certification_eligible': False})
+    return actions

+def frozen_world_evidence(cursor, *, project_id, root_operation_id):
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT b.book_id,b.book_design_version FROM authority_book_production b
+        JOIN authority_book_chapter_request r ON r.book_run_id=b.book_run_id
+        WHERE r.chapter_root_operation_id=%s AND b.project_id=%s""",
+        [root_operation_id, project_id],
+    )
+    book = cursor.fetchone()
+    if not book:
+        return None
+
+    def asset(version, expected_hash=None):
+        cursor.execute(
+            """SELECT v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_asset_version v
+            JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) WHERE v.asset_version_id=%s AND a.project_id=%s""",
+            [version, project_id],
+        )
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or expected_hash and row[0] != expected_hash:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "QUALITY_FOUNDATION_INPUT_DRIFT",
+                "Retain the frozen Book Foundation versions and hashes",
+            )
+        return {
+            "asset_version_id": version,
+            "content_hash": row[0],
+            "project_id": project_id,
+        }, row[1]
+
+    design_ref, design = asset(book[1])
+    if not design.get("foundation_set"):
+        return None
+    foundation = QualifiedFoundationSet.from_mapping(design["foundation_set"])
+    binding = next(
+        b for b in foundation.bindings if b.role is FoundationRole.WORLD_BIBLE
+    )
+    world_ref, world = asset(binding.asset_version_id, binding.content_hash)
+    receipt_ref, receipt = asset(
+        binding.qualification_evidence_asset_version_id, binding.qualification_hash
+    )
+    if (
+        world.get("book_id") != book[0]
+        or receipt.get("project_id") != project_id
+        or receipt.get("design_scope") != "WORLD_BIBLE"
+        or receipt.get("transition") != "APPROVE"
+        or receipt.get("asset_version_id") != binding.asset_version_id
+        or receipt.get("target_content_hash") != binding.content_hash
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "QUALITY_FOUNDATION_SCOPE_INVALID",
+            "An independent governed World design receipt must bind the exact Book input",
+        )
+    return {
+        "contract_version": CONTRACT,
+        "book_id": book[0],
+        "foundation_set_digest": foundation.set_digest,
+        "inputs": [design_ref, world_ref, receipt_ref],
+        "world_projection": {
+            "asset_version_id": binding.asset_version_id,
+            "content_hash": binding.content_hash,
+            "qualification_evidence_asset_version_id": binding.qualification_evidence_asset_version_id,
+            "qualification_hash": binding.qualification_hash,
+            "activation_id": binding.activation_id,
+            "rules": world.get("rules", []),
+            "constraints": world.get("constraints", []),
+            "technology_power": [
+                d
+                for d in world.get("dimensions", [])
+                if d.get("dimension") == "technology_power"
+            ],
+            "evidence_scope": "Independent governed design constraints and initial capability bounds. Not accepted past events. Empty prior chapter state at genesis is not a contradictory prior state.",
+        },
+    }
+
+
+def world_input_bindings(database_url, *, project_id, root_operation_id):
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        evidence = frozen_world_evidence(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=project_id, root_operation_id=root_operation_id
+        )
+    return evidence["inputs"] if evidence else []
+
+
+def request_world_projection(request):
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(request.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT root_operation_id FROM authority_operation WHERE operation_id=%s AND project_id=%s",
+            [request.operation_id, request.project_id],
+        )
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        evidence = (
+            frozen_world_evidence(
+                uow.cursor, project_id=request.project_id, root_operation_id=row[0]
+            )
+            if row
+            else None
+        )
+    if not evidence:
+        return None
+    frozen = {i.asset_version_id: i.content_hash for i in request.inputs}
+    if any(
+        frozen.get(i["asset_version_id"]) != i["content_hash"]
+        for i in evidence["inputs"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "QUALITY_FOUNDATION_INPUT_REQUIRED",
+            "Admit exact governed World evidence before assessment; do not read mutable unbound sources",
+        )
+    return evidence["world_projection"]
