+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import hashlib
+import json
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
+from uuid import uuid4
+
+from psycopg.types.json import Jsonb
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.operation_graph import (
+    GraphCommandContext,
+    _record,
+)
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.service import admit_command_in_uow
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+
+CONTRACT = "kernel-derived-step-successor.v1"
+
+
+def _settled_graph(cursor, root_id):
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_hash,e.status,r.status,r.accounting_basis,
+        r.evidence->>'cost_authorization_ref' FROM authority_external_effect e
+        JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id) LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id)
+        WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s ORDER BY e.external_effect_id""",
+        [root_id],
+    )
+    effects = cursor.fetchall()
+    for effect in effects:
+        if effect[3:5] != ("SETTLED", "ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT") or not effect[5]:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_UNSETTLED",
+                "Reconcile all membership effects before revalidation",
+            )
+        if effect[2] == "SUCCEEDED":
+            continue
+        if effect[2] not in {"FAILED_FINAL", "FAILED_RETRYABLE"}:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_UNSETTLED",
+                "Unknown or dispatched outcomes cannot authorize revalidation",
+            )
+        cursor.execute(
+            """SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_saga_step_successor j
+            JOIN authority_external_effect e ON e.operation_id=j.predecessor_operation_id
+            WHERE e.external_effect_id=%s AND j.contract_version='kernel-failed-step-successor.v1'
+            AND j.policy_evidence->>'contract_version'='chapter-provider-schema-contract-repair.v1'
+            AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(j.policy_evidence->'known_failed_effects') b
+                WHERE b->>0=e.external_effect_id AND b->>1=e.request_hash AND b->>2=e.status
+                  AND b->>3='SETTLED' AND b->>4='ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT' AND b->>5=%s))""",
+            [effect[0], effect[5]],
+        )
+        authorized_schema_failure = cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        cursor.execute('''SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect failed
+            JOIN authority_attempt a ON a.operation_id=failed.operation_id AND a.attempt_id=failed.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,attempt_id}'
+            JOIN authority_operation o ON o.operation_id=failed.operation_id
+            JOIN authority_attempt later ON later.operation_id=o.operation_id AND later.attempt_number>a.attempt_number
+            JOIN authority_external_effect success ON success.operation_id=later.operation_id AND success.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,attempt_id}'=later.attempt_id
+            JOIN authority_budget_reservation r ON r.external_effect_id=success.external_effect_id
+            WHERE failed.external_effect_id=%s AND o.status='SUCCEEDED' AND later.status='SUCCEEDED'
+              AND failed.status='FAILED_RETRYABLE' AND a.status='FAILED_RETRYABLE'
+              AND success.status='SUCCEEDED' AND r.status='SETTLED'
+              AND r.accounting_basis='ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT' AND r.evidence->>'cost_authorization_ref'=%s)''', [effect[0],effect[5]])
+        if not authorized_schema_failure and not cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_UNRESOLVED_FAILURE",
+                "Preserve unrelated failure recovery ownership",
+            )
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id)
+        JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id) WHERE o.root_operation_id=%s AND l.released_at IS NULL AND l.expires_at>NOW())""",
+        [root_id],
+    )
+    if cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "DERIVED_STEP_LIVE_LEASE", "Wait for the original execution owner"
+        )
+    return [list(e) for e in effects]
+
+
+def replace_derived_step(database_url, *, predecessor_id, context, policy_evidence):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(
+        json.dumps(
+            {"predecessor": predecessor_id, "policy": policy_evidence},
+            sort_keys=True,
+            default=str,
+        ).encode()
+    ).hexdigest()
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        c = uow.cursor
+        c.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        row = c.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_SOURCE_MISSING", "Retain the completed source operation"
+            )
+        source = row[0]
+        project = source["project_id"]
+        root_id = source["root_operation_id"]
+        if source["actor_id"] != context.actor_id or not set(
+            source["definition_snapshot"]["permissions"]
+        ).issubset(context.permissions):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PERMISSION_DENIED",
+                "Revalidation requires the original actor and permissions",
+            )
+        c.execute(
+            "SELECT request_digest,successor_operation_id FROM authority_saga_step_revalidation WHERE predecessor_operation_id=%s",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        replay = c.fetchone()
+        if replay:
+            if replay[0] != digest:
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT",
+                    "The frozen revalidation request changed",
+                )
+            return {"operation_id": replay[1], "replayed": True}
+        repo = uow.repositories.operation_graph
+        saga = repo.saga_for_update(source["saga_id"], project)
+        repo.operation(root_id, project, lock=True)
+        c.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(o),business_deadline_at FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s",
+            [root_id],
+        )
+        root, deadline = c.fetchone()
+        c.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(s) FROM authority_saga_step s WHERE saga_id=%s AND operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [saga["saga_id"], predecessor_id],
+        )
+        row = c.fetchone()
+        from novel_forge.modules.canon_graph.validation_recovery import (
+            selected_validation_policy,
+            canon_revalidation_evidence,
+        )
+
+        admission = selected_validation_policy(
+            c, project_id=project, root_operation_id=root_id, lock=True
+        )
+        if (
+            not row
+            or root["status"] != "WAITING_CHILDREN"
+            or root["actor_id"] != context.actor_id
+            or saga["status"] not in {"RUNNING", "FORWARD_RECOVERY"}
+            or admission != policy_evidence.get("admission")
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_FRONTIER_INVALID",
+                "Only the unaccepted current frontier and exact trusted v6 policy may be revalidated",
+            )
+        step = row[0]
+        if step["compensation_status"] != "NOT_REQUIRED" or step.get(
+            "compensation_operation_id"
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_COMPENSATION_PRESENT",
+                "Retain compensation recovery ownership",
+            )
+        c.execute(
+            """SELECT o.definition_name FROM authority_operation_dependency d JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id)
+            WHERE d.depends_on_operation_id=%s""",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        dependent_names = [r[0] for r in c.fetchall()]
+        quality_correction = policy_evidence.get('quality_revalidation')
+        if quality_correction:
+            from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.revalidation import quality_revalidation_evidence
+            if quality_revalidation_evidence(c,source) != quality_correction:
+                raise KernelError('DERIVED_STEP_QUALITY_PROOF_INVALID', 'Reproduce the exact frozen assessment correction')
+        allowed_dependents = {'chapter.quality.evaluate','chapter.publishing.prepare_release','chapter.export.render'} if quality_correction else set()
+        if any(
+            r
+            not in {
+                "chapter.quality.structural.evaluate",
+                "chapter.quality.commercial.evaluate",
+                "chapter.quality.source_originality.evaluate",
+                "chapter.quality.longform.evaluate",
+                "chapter.quality.platform.evaluate",
+            }
+            for r in dependent_names
+        ) and any(r not in allowed_dependents for r in dependent_names):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_DEPENDENT_COMMIT",
+                "Committed downstream work cannot be rebound",
+            )
+        c.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(a) FROM authority_attempt a WHERE operation_id=%s ORDER BY attempt_number",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        attempts = [r[0] for r in c.fetchall()]
+        c.execute(
+            """SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,a.asset_type,o.role FROM authority_operation_output_asset o
+            JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) JOIN authority_asset a ON a.asset_id=v.asset_id
+            WHERE o.operation_id=%s ORDER BY v.asset_version_id,o.role""",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        outputs = [list(r) for r in c.fetchall()]
+        effects = _settled_graph(c, root_id)
+        old = source["definition_snapshot"]
+        inputs = source["input_asset_snapshot"]
+        canonical_replacement = None
+        replacements = []
+        if quality_correction:
+            inputs = quality_correction['input_snapshot']
+            replacements = quality_correction['input_replacements']
+            target_version = quality_correction['target_version']
+        elif source["definition_name"] == "chapter.canon.update":
+            if (
+                source["status"] != "SUCCEEDED"
+                or source["definition_version"] != "3.9.0"
+            ):
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_CANON_SOURCE_INVALID",
+                    "Only the completed legacy pure validator can be revalidated",
+                )
+            c.execute(
+                "SELECT count(*) FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s",
+                [predecessor_id],
+            )
+            if c.fetchone()[0] or canon_revalidation_evidence(
+                c, source
+            ) != policy_evidence.get("diagnostic"):
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_CANON_PROOF_INVALID",
+                    "Reproduce the exact pure diagnostic without regenerating claims or prose",
+                )
+            target_version = "3.9.10"
+        elif source["definition_name"] in {
+            "chapter.quality.longform.evaluate",
+            "chapter.quality.commercial.evaluate",
+        }:

+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_QUALITY_SOURCE_INVALID",
+                    "Only reviews against the superseded Canon may be revalidated",
+                )
+            c.execute(
+                """SELECT j.predecessor_operation_id,j.successor_operation_id,j.policy_evidence,v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content
+                FROM authority_saga_step_revalidation j JOIN authority_saga_step s ON s.operation_id=j.successor_operation_id
+                JOIN authority_operation n ON n.operation_id=s.operation_id AND n.status='SUCCEEDED' AND n.definition_version='3.9.10'
+                JOIN authority_operation_output_asset o ON o.operation_id=n.operation_id AND o.role='primary'
+                JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+                WHERE j.saga_id=%s AND j.contract_version=%s AND j.policy_evidence #>> '{diagnostic,contract_version}'='chapter-canon-derived-revalidation.v1' """,
+                [saga["saga_id"], CONTRACT],
+            )
+            canon = c.fetchone()
+            from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.cost_routing import (
+                snapshot_hash,
+            )
+
+            if (
+                not canon
+                or snapshot_hash(canon[5])
+                != canon[2]["diagnostic"]["corrected_content_hash"]
+            ):
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_CANON_NOT_COMPLETED",
+                    "Run the exact new Canon validator before its affected reviews",
+                )
+            old_binding = canon[2]["diagnostic"]["source_output"]
+            new_binding = {"asset_version_id": canon[3], "content_hash": canon[4]}
+
+            def is_old_canon(binding):
+                return (
+                    binding.get("asset_version_id") == old_binding["asset_version_id"]
+                    and binding.get("content_hash") == old_binding["content_hash"]
+                    and binding.get("project_id") == project
+                )
+
+            if sum(is_old_canon(binding) for binding in inputs) != 1:
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_REVIEW_INPUT_DRIFT",
+                    "Review must consume the exact superseded Canon output",
+                )
+            inputs = [{**i, **new_binding} if is_old_canon(i) else i for i in inputs]
+            canonical_replacement = {
+                "predecessor_operation_id": canon[0],
+                "successor_operation_id": canon[1],
+                "old_input": old_binding,
+                "new_input": new_binding,
+            }
+            if canonical_replacement != policy_evidence.get(
+                "canonical_input_replacement"
+            ):
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "DERIVED_STEP_REVIEW_PROOF_INVALID",
+                    "Bind the exact completed Canon successor",

+                    quality_applicability_repair_evidence,

+        else:
+            raise KernelError(

+        quality_policy = {'3.9.11':('internal_quality_disposition','creative-quality-disposition.v2'),

+            '3.9.13':('internal_quality_input_contract','creative-quality-admission.v2')}

+            ("canon_validation_contract", "grounded-canon-validation.v2")

+            else ("quality_applicability_contract", "quality-host-applicability.v1")

+            raise KernelError(
+                "DERIVED_STEP_TARGET_INVALID",
+                "Select the explicit versioned correction",
+            )
+        duration = int(
+            root["definition_snapshot"]["timeout_policy"]["operation_timeout_seconds"]

+        if (
+            not deadline

+        graph = GraphCommandContext(

+            context.actor_id,
+            source["trace_id"],
+            source["correlation_id"],

+                successor,
+                mapped_dependency,
+                condition,
+            )
+        c.execute(
+            """INSERT INTO authority_saga_step_revalidation(revalidation_id,project_id,saga_id,predecessor_operation_id,
+            successor_operation_id,actor_id,contract_version,request_digest,source_step_snapshot,source_execution_snapshot,policy_evidence)
+            VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)""",
+            [
+                str(uuid4()),

+                context.actor_id,

+                digest,

+                    {
+                        "operation": source,
+                        "attempts": attempts,
+                        "outputs": outputs,
+                        "settled_graph_effects": effects,
+                    }
+                ),
+                Jsonb(policy_evidence),
+            ],
+        )
+        if target_version == '3.9.13' and source['status'] in {'ADMITTED','READY','FAILED_RETRYABLE'}:
+            from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.runtime import OperationTransitionCommand, transition_operation_in_uow
+            for before,after in ((source['status'],'CANCELLATION_REQUESTED'),('CANCELLATION_REQUESTED','CANCELLED')):

+            )

+                OperationTransitionCommand,

+def acknowledge_revalidated_effects(cursor, model_effects, operation_ids):
+    cursor.execute(
+        """SELECT j.predecessor_operation_id,j.successor_operation_id,j.source_execution_snapshot,j.policy_evidence
+        FROM authority_saga_step_revalidation j JOIN authority_operation n ON n.operation_id=j.successor_operation_id
+        JOIN authority_saga_step s ON s.operation_id=n.operation_id AND s.saga_id=j.saga_id
+        WHERE j.predecessor_operation_id=ANY(%s) AND j.contract_version=%s
+          AND n.status='SUCCEEDED' AND n.definition_version='3.9.9'
+          AND n.definition_name IN ('chapter.quality.longform.evaluate','chapter.quality.commercial.evaluate')""",
+        [operation_ids, CONTRACT],
+    )
+    for predecessor, successor, snapshot, policy in cursor.fetchall():
+        source = snapshot["operation"]
+        if (
+            source["definition_version"] != "3.9.0"
+            or source["status"] not in {"SUCCEEDED", "DEAD_LETTER"}
+            or not policy.get("canonical_input_replacement")
+        ):
+            continue
+        attempts = {a["attempt_id"]: a for a in snapshot["attempts"]}
+        effects = {e[0]: e for e in snapshot["settled_graph_effects"]}
+        for item in model_effects:
+            attempt = attempts.get(item["attempt_id"])
+            recorded = effects.get(item["external_effect_id"])
+            if (
+                item["operation_id"] != predecessor
+                or item["status"] != "SUCCEEDED"
+                or not attempt
+                or attempt["status"] != "FAILED_RETRYABLE"
+                or attempt["result"].get("error_code")
+                != "QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_CONFLICT"
+                or not recorded
+            ):
+                continue
+            cursor.execute(
+                """SELECT e.request_hash,e.status,r.status,r.accounting_basis,r.evidence->>'cost_authorization_ref'
+                FROM authority_external_effect e JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id)
+                WHERE e.external_effect_id=%s AND e.operation_id=%s""",
+                [item["external_effect_id"], predecessor],
+            )
+            current = cursor.fetchone()
+            if (
+                current
+                and list(current) == recorded[1:]
+                and current[1:4] == ("SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT")
+                and current[4]
+            ):
+                item["settled_candidate_superseded"] = True
+                item["successor_operation_id"] = successor

+def continue_candidate_execution_window(database_url, *, root_id, predecessor_id, target_version, book_run_id, principal,
+                                       clock=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc), start_repair_phase=False):
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        cursor = uow.cursor
+        cursor.execute('SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE', [root_id])
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            raise KernelError('OPERATION_NOT_FOUND', 'Missing continuation Root')
+        root = row[0]
+        principal.require('chapter.authority.execute', root['project_id'])
+        principal.require('book.authority.execute', root['project_id'])
+        if root['actor_id'] != principal.principal_id or root['status'] != 'WAITING_CHILDREN' or root['definition_name'] != 'chapter.slice.run':
+            raise KernelError('EXECUTION_WINDOW_ROOT_INVALID', 'Only an owned unfinished Chapter Root can continue')
+        cursor.execute('SELECT business_deadline_at FROM authority_operation WHERE operation_id=%s', [root_id])
+        deadline = cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        now = clock()
+        if not deadline or (deadline > now and not start_repair_phase):
+            return {'continued':False,'deadline':deadline}
+        cursor.execute('SELECT project_id,book_id,active_chapter_request_id,accepted_through_chapter FROM authority_book_production WHERE book_run_id=%s FOR UPDATE', [book_run_id])
+        book = cursor.fetchone()
+        if not book or book[0] != root['project_id']:
+            raise KernelError('EXECUTION_WINDOW_BOOK_INVALID','Continuation must bind the exact Book')
+        cursor.execute('SELECT chapter_root_operation_id,chapter_number FROM authority_book_chapter_request WHERE chapter_request_id=%s', [book[2]])
+        if cursor.fetchone() != (root_id,book[3]+1):
+            raise KernelError('EXECUTION_WINDOW_FRONTIER_INVALID','Only the current unaccepted Chapter can continue')
+        admission = selected_book_template(cursor,project_id=book[0],book_id=book[1])
+        template = selected_template(admission['policy_snapshot']['policy'],book[1]) if admission else None
+        if not template or template.get('chapter_contract_recovery') != 'KNOWN_SETTLED_CONTRACT_REPAIR':
+            raise KernelError('EXECUTION_WINDOW_POLICY_REQUIRED','An explicit trusted creative continuation policy is required')
+        if any(key in root['command_payload'] for key in ('business_deadline_at','author_deadline_at')):
+            raise KernelError('EXECUTION_WINDOW_AUTHOR_DEADLINE','An author deadline cannot be extended by execution recovery')
+        cursor.execute("SELECT o.status,a.result->>'error_code',o.actor_id,o.definition_name FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_attempt a USING(operation_id) JOIN authority_saga_step s USING(operation_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.root_operation_id=%s ORDER BY a.attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1", [predecessor_id,root_id])
+        source = cursor.fetchone()
+        corrections = {
+            **{(f'chapter.quality.{g}.evaluate','DEAD_LETTER','QUALITY_APPLICABILITY_CONFLICT'):('3.9.9','quality_applicability_contract','quality-host-applicability.v1') for g in ('structural','commercial','source_originality','longform','platform')},
+            ('chapter.canon.propose_changes','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED','CANON_GROUNDING_REEXTRACTION_REQUIRED'):('3.9.8','support_kind_contract','canon-support-kinds.v2'),
+            ('chapter.draft.generate','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED','REALIZATION_EPISTEMIC_SCOPE_VIOLATION'):('3.9.2','scene_information_flow','scene-information-flow.v1'),
+            ('chapter.review.evaluate','FAILED_FINAL','REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_INVALID'):('3.9.3','review_knowledge_scope','delivered-card-scope.v1'),
+            ('chapter.review.evaluate','FAILED_FINAL','REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_REQUIRED'):('3.9.3','review_knowledge_scope','delivered-card-scope.v1'),
+            ('chapter.revision.apply','CANCELLATION_REQUESTED','REVISION_OUTSIDE_DIAGNOSIS_SCOPE'):('3.9.4','chapter_length_scope','chapter-length-repair.v1'),
+            ('chapter.revision.apply','FAILED_RETRYABLE','CHAPTER_LENGTH_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE'):('3.9.4','chapter_length_scope','chapter-length-repair.v1'),
+            ('chapter.revision.apply','FAILED_RETRYABLE','OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT'):('3.9.4','chapter_length_scope','chapter-length-repair.v1'),
+            ('chapter.canon.extract','FAILED_FINAL','MODEL_EFFECT_FINAL_PROVIDER_SCHEMA_REJECTED'):('3.9.7','canon_wire_projection','codex-canonical-json.v1'),

+def continued_attempt_limit(cursor, *, operation_id, project_id, original_limit):
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT j.payload FROM audit_log j JOIN authority_operation o ON o.root_operation_id=j.operation_id
+        JOIN authority_attempt a ON a.operation_id=o.operation_id
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.status IN ('READY','FAILED_RETRYABLE')
+        AND o.definition_name='chapter.revision.apply' AND o.definition_version='3.9.4'
+        AND j.action=%s AND j.payload->>'contract_version'='chapter-execution-window-renewal.v3'
+        AND j.payload->>'predecessor_operation_id'=o.operation_id
+        AND j.payload->>'failed_attempt_id'=a.attempt_id AND a.status='FAILED_RETRYABLE'
+        AND a.result->>'error_code'='OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT'
+        AND a.attempt_number=(SELECT max(attempt_number) FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=o.operation_id)
+        AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect e WHERE e.operation_id=o.operation_id AND e.request_payload #>> '{_production_execution,attempt_id}'=a.attempt_id)
+        ORDER BY j.created_at DESC LIMIT 1""",[operation_id,project_id,EVENT])
+    row = cursor.fetchone()
+    if not row:
+        return original_limit
+    evidence = row[0]
+    if evidence.get('original_attempt_limit') != original_limit or evidence.get('control_recovery_attempt_limit') != original_limit+1:
+        return original_limit
+    return original_limit+1

+def read_rejected_structured_completion(database_url, context, *, effect_id):
+    _validate_effect_context(context)
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT status,actor_id FROM authority_operation WHERE operation_id=%s AND project_id=%s",
+            [context.operation_id, context.project_id],
+        )
+        owner = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not owner or owner != ("FAILED_FINAL", context.actor_id):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "EFFECT_REJECTED_RESULT_OWNER_INVALID",
+                "Require this actor and its failed-final owner",
+            )
+        effect = uow.repositories.external_effects.get_for_update(effect_id)
+        _require_project(effect, context.project_id)
+        if effect["operation_id"] != context.operation_id:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "EFFECT_OPERATION_CONFLICT",
+                "Rejected result belongs to another Operation",
+            )
+        if (
+            effect["status"] != "FAILED_FINAL"
+            or effect["effect_type"] != "model.invoke"
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "EFFECT_REJECTED_RESULT_STATUS_INVALID",
+                "Only the original failed model dispatch may be revalidated",
+            )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT d.status,d.evidence,d.payload_hash,d.generation,e.last_error FROM authority_external_effect_dispatch d JOIN authority_external_effect e USING(external_effect_id) WHERE d.external_effect_id=%s ORDER BY d.generation DESC LIMIT 1",
+            [effect_id],
+        )
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if (
+            not row
+            or row[0] != "FAILED_FINAL"

+        ):
+            raise KernelError(

+                "Failed dispatch evidence must retain its exact digest",

+        result = evidence.get("production_execution", {}).get("execution_result", {})

+            process.get("failure_code") != "OUTPUT_SCHEMA_FAILURE"
+            or result.get("outcome") != "FINAL_FAILURE"

+            [
+                context.operation_id,
+                effect_id,
+                context.operation_id,
+                context.operation_id,

+        )
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]:

+                "EFFECT_REJECTED_RESULT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
+                "All original calls and usage must be resolved without an active lease",

+            "request_payload": effect["request_payload"],

+        }

+def has_candidate_domain_outputs(cursor, operation_id):
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT a.asset_type,o.role FROM authority_operation_output_asset o
+        JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s""", [operation_id])
+    contract_types = {'capability_materialization_manifest','execution_commit_policy','execution_context_policy',
+        'execution_source_policy','execution_validation_policy','fallback_policy','input_manifest','model_profile',
+        'prompt_bundle','provider_request_admission_manifest','route_policy','route_snapshot','skill_bundle','tool_permission_snapshot'}
+    allowed = {(t,'execution_contract') for t in contract_types}
+    allowed |= {('execution_evidence',r) for r in ('cost-receipt','normalized-output','output-manifest','process-receipt',
+        'raw-output','usage-receipt','validation-manifest')}
+    allowed |= {('cost_ledger','cost_evidence'),('execution_recovery_session','execution_recovery_projection'),
+        ('model_call_log','model_evidence'),('model_call_log','model_failure_evidence'),('routing_decision','routing_evidence')}
+    return any(tuple(row) not in allowed for row in cursor.fetchall())

+def replace_failed_step(
+    database_url,
+    *,
+    predecessor_id,
+    target_version,
+    context: AdmissionContext,
+    policy_evidence,
+    expected_failure_code,
+    settled_candidate_effects=(),
+    transaction_guard=None,
+):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256(
+        json.dumps(
+            {
+                "predecessor": predecessor_id,
+                "target": target_version,
+                "policy": policy_evidence,
+                "failure_code": expected_failure_code,
+                **({"settled_candidate_effects": settled_candidate_effects} if settled_candidate_effects else {}),
+            },
+            sort_keys=True,
+            default=str,
+        ).encode()
+    ).hexdigest()
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        cursor = uow.cursor
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_SOURCE_MISSING", "Missing native predecessor"
+            )
+        source = row[0]
+        project = source["project_id"]
+        if source["actor_id"] != context.actor_id:
+            raise KernelError("STEP_SUCCESSOR_SOURCE_INVALID", "Recovery requires the original actor")
+        if not set(source["definition_snapshot"]["permissions"]).issubset(context.permissions):
+            raise KernelError("PERMISSION_DENIED", "Recovery requires the original command permissions")
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT request_digest,successor_operation_id FROM authority_saga_step_successor WHERE predecessor_operation_id=%s",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        existing = cursor.fetchone()
+        if existing:
+            if existing[0] != digest:
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "STEP_SUCCESSOR_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", "Recovery command changed"
+                )
+            return {"operation_id": existing[1], "replayed": True}
+        if transaction_guard is not None:
+            transaction_guard(uow)
+        repo = uow.repositories.operation_graph
+        saga = repo.saga_for_update(source["saga_id"], project)
+        repo.operation(source["root_operation_id"], project, lock=True)
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM authority_operation o WHERE operation_id=%s",
+            [source["root_operation_id"]],
+        )
+        root = cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(s) FROM authority_saga_step s WHERE operation_id=%s AND saga_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [predecessor_id, saga["saga_id"]],
+        )
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        final_review_repair = bool(settled_candidate_effects and policy_evidence.get("contract_version") == "chapter-review-output-repair.v1"
+            and source["definition_name"] == "chapter.review.evaluate" and source["definition_version"] == "3.9.0"
+            and expected_failure_code in {"REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_INVALID", "REVIEW_KNOWLEDGE_CHECK_REQUIRED"})
+        canon_protocol_repair = policy_evidence.get("contract_version") == "chapter-provider-schema-contract-repair.v1"
+        quality_contract_repair = policy_evidence.get("contract_version")=="chapter-quality-contract-repair.v1"
+        canon_support_repair = policy_evidence.get("contract_version") == "chapter-support-contract-repair.v1"
+        canon_capacity_repair = policy_evidence.get("contract_version") == "chapter-canon-capacity-repair.v1"
+        if (
+            not row
+            or source["status"] != ("CANCELLATION_REQUESTED" if canon_capacity_repair or settled_candidate_effects and not final_review_repair and not quality_contract_repair else "DEAD_LETTER" if quality_contract_repair else "FAILED_FINAL")
+            or root["status"] != "WAITING_CHILDREN"
+            or saga["status"] not in ({"RUNNING", "FORWARD_RECOVERY"} if canon_capacity_repair or canon_protocol_repair or canon_support_repair or quality_contract_repair else {"RUNNING"})
+            or source["actor_id"] != context.actor_id
+            or root["actor_id"] != context.actor_id
+            or source["definition_version"] == target_version
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_SOURCE_INVALID",
+                f"Only an owned failed child of a live graph can be replaced (step={bool(row)}, source={source['status']}, root={root['status']}, saga={saga['status']}, source_version={source['definition_version']}, target={target_version})",
+            )
+        step = row[0]
+        if step["compensation_status"] != "NOT_REQUIRED" or step.get(
+            "compensation_operation_id"
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_COMPENSATION_PRESENT",
+                "Compensation retains its original recovery owner",
+            )
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT business_deadline_at FROM authority_operation WHERE operation_id=%s",
+            [root["operation_id"]],
+        )
+        deadline = cursor.fetchone()[0]
+        if deadline and deadline <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "OPERATION_BUSINESS_TIMEOUT",
+                "The frozen Root deadline remains authoritative",
+            )
+        cursor.execute(
+            """SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s),
+            EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_operation_output_asset WHERE operation_id=%s),
+            EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_operation_dependency WHERE depends_on_operation_id=%s),
+            EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id)
+                WHERE a.operation_id=%s AND l.released_at IS NULL AND l.expires_at>NOW())""",
+            [predecessor_id] * 4,
+        )
+        external, output, dependent, live_lease = cursor.fetchone()
+        if settled_candidate_effects or canon_capacity_repair or canon_protocol_repair:
+            output = has_candidate_domain_outputs(cursor, predecessor_id)
+        if external and canon_protocol_repair:
+            cursor.execute("""SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_hash,e.status,r.status,r.accounting_basis,r.evidence->>'cost_authorization_ref'
+                FROM authority_external_effect e LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id)
+                WHERE e.operation_id=%s ORDER BY e.external_effect_id""",[predecessor_id])
+            effects=cursor.fetchall()
+            if not effects or effects != [tuple(r) for r in policy_evidence.get('known_failed_effects',[])]:
+                raise KernelError('STEP_SUCCESSOR_PROTOCOL_EVIDENCE_MISMATCH','Bind the exact recorded failed membership effects')
+            if any(r[2] not in {'FAILED_FINAL','FAILED_RETRYABLE'} or r[3:5]!=('SETTLED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT') or not r[5] for r in effects):
+                raise KernelError('STEP_SUCCESSOR_PROTOCOL_UNSETTLED','Unknown outcomes or priced accounts cannot use membership Schema succession')
+            external=False
+        if external and settled_candidate_effects:
+            cursor.execute("SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_hash,e.status,r.status,e.effect_type FROM authority_external_effect e LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id) WHERE e.operation_id=%s ORDER BY e.external_effect_id", [predecessor_id])
+            effects = cursor.fetchall()
+            if effects != [(effect, digest, "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "model.invoke") for effect, digest in sorted(settled_candidate_effects)]:
+                raise KernelError("STEP_SUCCESSOR_CANDIDATE_UNSETTLED", "Only exact known succeeded settled candidates may be superseded")
+            external = False
+        if external or output or dependent or live_lease:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_EXTERNAL_OR_DEPENDENT_WORK",
+                "Effects, outputs, dependents or leases prevent step succession",
+            )
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT to_jsonb(a) FROM authority_attempt a WHERE operation_id=%s ORDER BY attempt_number",
+            [predecessor_id],
+        )
+        attempts = [r[0] for r in cursor.fetchall()]
+        if (
+            not attempts
+            or attempts[-1]["result"].get("error_code") != expected_failure_code
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_FAILURE_MISMATCH",
+                "Recovery must bind the exact recorded local failure",
+            )
+        target = uow.repositories.definitions.get(
+            source["definition_name"], target_version
+        )
+        old_definition = source["definition_snapshot"]
+        if (
+            not target
+            or target["handler_key"] != old_definition["handler_key"]
+            or target["permissions"] != old_definition["permissions"]
+            or target["completion_contract"] != old_definition["completion_contract"]
+            or target["command_schema"] != old_definition["command_schema"]
+            or target["result_schema"] != old_definition["result_schema"]
+            or target["external_capabilities"]
+            != old_definition["external_capabilities"]
+        ):

+                "STEP_SUCCESSOR_CONTRACT_CHANGED",
+                "Successor cannot relax completion, permissions or external execution requirements",

+        if canon_capacity_repair or canon_protocol_repair or canon_support_repair or quality_contract_repair:

+            admission = selected_book_template(cursor, project_id=project, book_id=book[0]) if book else None
+            template = selected_template(admission['policy_snapshot']['policy'],book[0]) if admission else None

+        if quality_contract_repair:

-def codex_output_schema(schema: dict) -> dict:
+CODEX_CANONICAL_JSON_SCHEMA_REF = "schema:chapter-provider-output:canon_extraction:v25"
+CODEX_CANONICAL_JSON_CODEC = "codex-canonical-json.v1"
+
+
+def codex_output_schema(schema: dict, *, schema_ref: str = "") -> dict:
+    if schema_ref == CODEX_CANONICAL_JSON_SCHEMA_REF:
+        return {"type":"object","properties":{"output_json":{"type":"string"}},
+            "required":["output_json"],"additionalProperties":False}
+    return _codex_output_schema(schema, all_nullable=True)
+
+
+def strict_codex_json_loads(text: str):
+    def pairs(items):
+        result={}
+        for key,item in items:
+            if key in result:
+                raise ValueError("canonical JSON transport rejects duplicate keys")
+            result[key]=item
+        return result
+    def reject_constant(value):
+        raise ValueError("canonical JSON transport rejects non-finite numbers")
+    return json.loads(text,object_pairs_hook=pairs,parse_constant=reject_constant)
+
+
+def decode_codex_output(value, *, schema_ref: str):
+    if schema_ref != CODEX_CANONICAL_JSON_SCHEMA_REF:
+        return value, None
+    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value)!={"output_json"} or not isinstance(value['output_json'],str):
+        raise ValueError("canonical JSON transport requires exactly one output_json string")
+    decoded=strict_codex_json_loads(value['output_json'])
+    if not isinstance(decoded,dict):
+        raise ValueError("canonical JSON transport requires an object result")
+    def digest(item):
+        return 'sha256:'+hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(item,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False).encode()).hexdigest()
+    return decoded, {'contract_version':CODEX_CANONICAL_JSON_CODEC,'envelope_hash':digest(value),'decoded_hash':digest(decoded)}
+

+def codex_wire_correction_evidence(process_evidence: Mapping) -> dict | None:
+    contract = process_evidence.get('provider_schema_contract') or {}
+    canonical = process_evidence.get('output_schema')

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+
+REPAIR_VERSION = "native-planning-repair-evidence.v2"
+STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION = "native-planning-repair-evidence.v3"
+LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION = "native-planning-repair-evidence.v1"
+REPAIRABLE_FAILURES = {"SCHEMA_REJECTION", "MALFORMED_STRUCTURED_OUTPUT"}
+
+
+def native_planning_repair(cursor, *, project_id, book_id, operation_id, task_type, lineage, evidence_version=REPAIR_VERSION):
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.world_result_recovery import WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS
+    if evidence_version not in {REPAIR_VERSION,LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION,STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION}:
+        raise KernelError("NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_VERSION_INVALID","Repair evidence requires an exact supported version")
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT o.command_payload->'payload',g.input_snapshot_hash,g.input_snapshot
+        FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g USING(operation_id)
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND g.book_id=%s
+          AND (g.generator_operation_name=%s OR (%s='world_candidates.generate'
+            AND o.command_payload #>> '{payload,repair_result_diagnostic,error_code}'=ANY(%s)))
+          AND o.definition_name='story_planning.generate'""",
+        [operation_id,project_id,book_id,task_type,task_type,sorted(WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS)])
+    current = cursor.fetchone()
+    if not current:
+        return None
+    command, input_hash, inputs = current
+    world_repair = (command.get('repair_result_diagnostic') or {}).get('error_code') in WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS
+    if world_repair and task_type != 'world_candidates.generate':
+        return None
+    candidate = command.get("repair_of_candidate_id")
+    raw = command.get("repair_result_asset_version_id")
+    number = command.get("repair_number",0)
+    if not candidate and not raw:
+        return None
+    if bool(candidate) == bool(raw) or type(number) is not int or not 1 <= number <= (4 if evidence_version==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION else 2):
+        raise KernelError("NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_INVALID", "Repair requires one bounded native result identity")
+    result_id = candidate or raw
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT o.operation_id,o.status,g.repair_number,v.content_hash,e.external_effect_id,
+            e.request_payload #> '{_production_execution,capability_runtime,routing_decision}',
+            g.input_snapshot_hash,g.input_snapshot
+        FROM authority_operation_output_asset output JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+        LEFT JOIN authority_planning_result_admission recovery ON recovery.candidate_asset_version_id=output.asset_version_id
+          AND recovery.recovery_operation_id=output.operation_id
+        JOIN authority_operation o ON o.operation_id=COALESCE(recovery.source_operation_id,output.operation_id)
+        JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g ON g.operation_id=o.operation_id
+        JOIN authority_external_effect e ON e.operation_id=o.operation_id AND (e.status='SUCCEEDED' OR (%s AND e.status='FAILED_FINAL' AND recovery.external_effect_id=e.external_effect_id))
+          AND e.external_effect_id=COALESCE(v.content #>> '{execution_evidence,external_receipt_ref}',
+              v.content->>'external_effect_id',v.content #>> '{contract,evidence,external_receipt_ref}')
+        JOIN authority_budget_reservation r ON r.external_effect_id=e.external_effect_id AND r.status='SETTLED'
+        WHERE output.asset_version_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND g.book_id=%s
+          AND g.generator_operation_name=%s AND g.logical_key=%s
+          AND e.request_payload->>'stage'=%s
+          AND (%s AND g.candidate_asset_version_id=%s AND (o.status='SUCCEEDED' OR %s AND recovery.source_operation_id=o.operation_id)
+               OR NOT %s AND o.operation_id=%s AND g.candidate_asset_version_id IS NULL AND o.status='FAILED_FINAL')""",
+        [evidence_version==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION,result_id,project_id,book_id,command.get('generator_operation_name',task_type),command['logical_key'],task_type,
+         bool(candidate),result_id,evidence_version==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION,bool(candidate),command.get('repair_of_operation_id','')])
+    parents = cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(parents) != 1:
+        raise KernelError("NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID", "Repair must bind its exact settled native parent result and unchanged inputs")
+    parent = parents[0]
+    stage_number = None
+    if evidence_version==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION:
+        from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.stage_repairs import stage_ordinal
+        stage_number = stage_ordinal(cursor,lineage=lineage[:-1],stage=task_type)+1
+        if stage_number>2:
+            raise KernelError('NATIVE_PLANNING_STAGE_REPAIR_EXHAUSTED','This stage exhausted its bounded repair allowance')
+        if candidate:
+            from novel_forge.modules.story_design_authority.creative_validation import candidate_validation
+            proof=candidate_validation(cursor,project_id=project_id,asset_version_id=result_id)
+            if not proof or proof['generation_operation_id']!=parent[0] or proof['book_id']!=book_id:
+                raise KernelError('NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID','Require native creative candidate provenance')
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.domain import canonical_hash
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.application import _replay_generation_input_snapshot
+    frozen_inputs = _replay_generation_input_snapshot(inputs,parent[7],parent[6])
+    if (canonical_hash(inputs) != input_hash
+            or evidence_version == LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION and input_hash != parent[6]):
+        raise KernelError("NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID", "Repair input evidence differs from its native parent")
+    if parent[0] not in lineage or lineage[-1] != operation_id or number != parent[2]+1:
+        raise KernelError("NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_LINEAGE_INVALID", "Repair counter and lineage differ from the native parent")
+    from .execution_template import verify_template_provenance
+    policy = verify_template_provenance(cursor,parent[5])
+    if not policy or policy['mode'] != 'CREATIVE_VALIDATION' or policy['require_literary_qualification']:
+        return None
+    selected = parent[5]['selected']['candidate']
+    return {'version':evidence_version,'operation_id':operation_id,'parent_operation_id':parent[0],
+        'source_result_asset_version_id':result_id,'source_result_hash':parent[3],
+        'settled_parent_effect_id':parent[4],'input_snapshot_hash':input_hash,'repair_number':number,
+        'parent_candidate_id':selected['candidate_id'],'lineage_operation_ids':list(lineage),
+        **({'repair_stage':task_type,'stage_repair_number':stage_number} if stage_number is not None else {}),
+        **({'parent_input_snapshot_hash':parent[6],
+            'input_scope_hash':canonical_hash({item['role']:item for item in frozen_inputs})}
+            if evidence_version in {REPAIR_VERSION,STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION} else {})}

+def repair_route_admission(snapshot, row, template):
+    policy = template.get('repair_routing')
+    evidence = snapshot['task'].get('declared_requirements',{}).get('native_planning_repair')
+    if (not policy or not evidence or evidence.get('version') not in {REPAIR_VERSION,LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION,STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION}
+            or evidence.get('operation_id') != snapshot['task']['operation_id']
+            or evidence.get('lineage_operation_ids') != snapshot['task'].get('lineage_operation_ids')
+            or type(evidence.get('repair_number')) is not int
+            or not 1 <= (evidence.get('stage_repair_number',0) if policy['version']=='native-repair-routing.v2' else evidence['repair_number']) <= policy['max_same_route_repairs']
+            or (policy['version']=='native-repair-routing.v2') != (evidence.get('version')==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION)
+            or evidence.get('version')==STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION and evidence.get('repair_stage')!=snapshot['task']['task_type']
+            or evidence.get('parent_candidate_id') != row['candidate']['candidate_id']):
+        return None
+    previous = [h for h in snapshot['task']['history'] if h.get('candidate_id') == row['candidate']['candidate_id']]
+    failures = [h.get('failure_class') for h in previous if h.get('failure_class')]
+    if (not failures or any(f not in REPAIRABLE_FAILURES for f in failures)
+            or any(h.get('failure_class') and h['failure_class'] not in REPAIRABLE_FAILURES
+                for h in snapshot['task']['history'])
+            or len(previous) > evidence.get('stage_repair_number',evidence['repair_number'])):
+        return None
+    superseded = [reason for reason in row['rejected_reasons'] if reason in {
+        'FAILED_ROUTE_EXCLUDED','SEMANTIC_ARBITRATION_REQUIRED','ROUTE_RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED'}]
+    return {'version':policy['version'],'status':'BOUNDED_REPAIR_ADMITTED',
+        'evidence':evidence,'retained_failed_outcomes':previous,'superseded_route_reasons':superseded,
+        'candidate_contract_acceptance':'REQUIRED_AFTER_REPAIR','literary_qualification':'NOT_RUN'}
+
+
+def verify_native_repair(cursor, decision):
+    evidence = decision['task'].get('declared_requirements',{}).get('native_planning_repair')
+    if evidence is None:
+        return
+    task = decision['task']
+    current = native_planning_repair(cursor,project_id=task['project_id'],book_id=task['work_id'],
+        operation_id=task['operation_id'],task_type=task['task_type'],lineage=tuple(task['lineage_operation_ids']),
+        evidence_version=evidence['version'])
+    if current != evidence:
+        raise KernelError('NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_EVIDENCE_DRIFT','Frozen repair admission must retain its native source and counter')

+def han_characters(text: str) -> int:
+    import unicodedata
+    return sum(unicodedata.name(char, '').startswith(('CJK UNIFIED IDEOGRAPH-', 'CJK COMPATIBILITY IDEOGRAPH-')) for char in text)
+
+
+def freeze_chapter_length_scope(source: dict[str, Any], *, source_asset_version_id: str,
+                                context: dict[str, Any], context_asset_version_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.context_views import compile_platform_policy
+    from novel_forge.modules.review.diagnostics import prose_hash
+    profiles = []
+    for segment in context.get('context_segments', []):
+        if segment.get('asset_type') != 'platform_profile':
+            continue
+        profile_id = segment['asset_version_id']
+        rules = compile_platform_policy(profile_asset_version_id=profile_id, segment=segment).chapter_length_rules
+        if rules.get('unit') == 'HAN_CHARACTERS_V1':
+            profiles.append((segment, rules))
+    if not profiles:
+        return None
+    if len(profiles) != 1:
+        raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_SCOPE_PROFILE_CONFLICT', 'Length correction requires one exact frozen Platform profile')
+    segment, rules = profiles[0]
+    minimum, maximum = rules.get('minimum'), rules.get('maximum')
+    if type(minimum) is not int or type(maximum) is not int or not 0 < minimum <= maximum:
+        raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_POLICY_INVALID', 'Frozen length bounds must be positive ordered integers')
+    text = str(source.get('text') or '')
+    observed = han_characters(text)
+    if minimum <= observed <= maximum:
+        return None
+    return {'contract_version':'chapter-length-repair.v1', 'scope':'WHOLE_CHAPTER_LENGTH_CORRECTION',
+        'source_asset_version_id':source_asset_version_id, 'source_text_hash':prose_hash(text),
+        'context_asset_version_id':context_asset_version_id, 'platform_profile_asset_version_id':segment['asset_version_id'],
+        'platform_profile_content_hash':segment.get('content_digest'), 'unit':'HAN_CHARACTERS_V1',
+        'minimum':minimum, 'maximum':maximum, 'source_han_characters':observed,
+        'preservation':'FROZEN_SCENES_FACTS_VOICE_QUALITY_AND_REVIEW_REQUIRED'}
+
+
+def validate_chapter_length_scope(scope: dict[str, Any], source: dict[str, Any], text: str, *,
+                                  source_asset_version_id: str, context_asset_version_id: str) -> None:
+    from novel_forge.modules.review.diagnostics import prose_hash
+    original = str(source.get('text') or '')
+    minimum, maximum = scope.get('minimum'), scope.get('maximum')
+    if (scope.get('contract_version') != 'chapter-length-repair.v1' or scope.get('unit') != 'HAN_CHARACTERS_V1'
+        or scope.get('scope') != 'WHOLE_CHAPTER_LENGTH_CORRECTION'
+        or scope.get('source_asset_version_id') != source_asset_version_id or scope.get('context_asset_version_id') != context_asset_version_id
+        or scope.get('source_text_hash') != prose_hash(original) or scope.get('source_han_characters') != han_characters(original)
+        or type(minimum) is not int or type(maximum) is not int or not 0 < minimum <= maximum
+        or minimum <= han_characters(original) <= maximum):
+        raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_SCOPE_INVALID', 'Only an exact out-of-bounds frozen candidate authorizes whole-chapter length repair')
+    if not minimum <= han_characters(text) <= maximum:
+        raise KernelError('CHAPTER_LENGTH_REPAIR_INCOMPLETE', f'Actual HAN_CHARACTERS_V1 count {han_characters(text)} is outside frozen bounds {minimum}–{maximum}; repair the full candidate without padding')

+NAME = "story_planning.gateway_result.revalidate"

+def literal_null_paths(schema, value, path="$"):
+    paths = [path] if schema.get("type") == "null" and value is None else []
+    for branch in schema.get("anyOf", []):
+        paths.extend(literal_null_paths(branch, value, path))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        for key, child in schema.get("properties", {}).items():
+            if key in value:
+                paths.extend(literal_null_paths(child, value[key], path + "." + key))
+    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(schema.get("items"), dict):
+        for index, item in enumerate(value):
+            paths.extend(literal_null_paths(schema["items"], item, f"{path}[{index}]"))
+    return sorted(set(paths))
+

+def retained_output(query, *, project_id, provider, request):
+    production = provider["production_execution"]
+    result = production["execution_result"]
+
+    def read(reference, kind):
+        if not reference.startswith("authority-asset-version:"):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_EVIDENCE_INVALID",
+                "Require original Authority-backed process evidence",
+            )
+        document = query.require(
+            project_id=project_id, asset_version_id=reference.split(":", 1)[1]
+        )
+        content = document.content
+        payload = content.get("payload")
+        if (
+            document.asset_type != "execution_evidence"
+            or content.get("kind") != kind
+            or content.get("execution_id") != request["execution_id"]
+            or not isinstance(payload, dict)
+            or content.get("payload_hash")
+            != canonical_hash(payload).removeprefix("sha256:")
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_EVIDENCE_INVALID",
+                "Retain exact process and rejected-output payload digests",
+            )
+        return document, payload
+
+    process_doc, process = read(result["process_receipt_ref"], "process-receipt")
+    raw_doc, metadata = read(result["raw_output_ref"], "rejected-output")
+    if (
+        process.get("status") != "SUCCEEDED"
+        or process.get("returncode") != 0
+        or process.get("result_integrity") != "VALID"
+        or process.get("operation_id") != request["operation_id"]
+        or process.get("attempt_id") != request["attempt_id"]
+        or process.get("fencing_token") != request["fencing_token"]
+        or not process.get("authority_bound")
+        or metadata.get("validation") != "REJECTED"
+        or metadata.get("generation_allowed") is not False
+        or metadata.get("result_path") != process.get("result_path")
+        or metadata.get("input_sha256") != process.get("input_sha256")
+        or process.get("input_sha256")
+        != provider["capability_runtime"]["provider_view"]["component_hashes"][
+            "fully_rendered_prompt"
+        ].removeprefix("sha256:")
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_PROCESS_INVALID",
+            "Only an intact successful frozen process can be locally revalidated",
+        )
+    layout = ExecutionDirectoryLayout(
+        request["execution_id"], Path(process["execution_directory"])
+    )
+    receipt = ProcessReceipt.from_dict(process)
+    PersistentExecutionDirectory._validate_receipt_paths(layout, receipt)
+    path = Path(process["result_path"])
+    if (
+        path.resolve(strict=True) != path
+        or path.is_symlink()
+        or layout.receipt_path.resolve(strict=True) != layout.receipt_path
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_PATH_INVALID",
+            "Retained result and receipt must remain in their original native directory",
+        )
+    current = json.loads(layout.receipt_path.read_text())
+    identity = json.loads(layout.metadata_path.read_text())
+    if (
+        current != process
+        or identity.get("execution_id") != request["execution_id"]
+        or identity.get("request_digest") != process.get("request_digest")
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_PROCESS_DRIFT",
+            "The retained native process identity changed",
+        )
+    data = path.read_bytes()
+    if (
+        len(data) != metadata["byte_size"]
+        or "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != metadata["result_sha256"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_HASH_DRIFT", "The original rejected output changed"
+        )
+    raw = json.loads(data)
+    schema = production["process_evidence"]["output_schema"]
+    if canonical_hash(schema) != provider["capability_runtime"]["output_schema_hash"]:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_SCHEMA_DRIFT",
+            "Revalidate only the exact original full Schema",
+        )
+    paths = literal_null_paths(schema, raw)
+    if not paths:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED",
+            "This recovery corrects only the known literal-null engine rejection",
+        )
+    from novel_forge.api.authority_dependencies import (
+        validate_production_structured_result,
+    )
+
+    normalized = validate_production_structured_result(
+        production["process_evidence"]["stage"],
+        schema,
+        raw,
+        output_schema_ref=production["process_evidence"]["output_schema_version"],
+    )
+    if normalized != raw:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_NORMALIZATION_FORBIDDEN",
+            "Local recovery must preserve the complete Provider result",
+        )
+    return raw, paths, process_doc, raw_doc
+

+def recover(application, *, command, operation_id, principal):
+    principal.require("story_planning.authority.execute", command.project_id)
+    if command.generator_operation_name not in {
+        "book_plan.generate",
+        "volume_plan.generate",
+        "arc_plan.generate",
+        "chapter_group_plan.generate",
+        "chapter_plan.generate",
+    }:
+        return None
+    if (
+        principal.principal_id != command.actor_id
+        and principal.principal_type is not PrincipalType.LOCAL_OPERATOR
+    ):
+        raise PermissionError(
+            "Recovery requires the actual source actor or a scoped trusted operator"
+        )
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT o.status,o.command_payload->'payload',g.input_snapshot,g.version,g.candidate_asset_version_id FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g USING(operation_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s",
+            [operation_id, command.project_id],
+        )
+        source = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        from .application import _command_payload
+
+        if (
+            not source
+            or source[0] != "FAILED_FINAL"
+            or source[4]
+            or source[1] != _command_payload(command)
+        ):
+            return None
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT e.external_effect_id,e.request_payload,e.last_error FROM authority_external_effect e WHERE e.operation_id=%s AND e.status='FAILED_FINAL' AND e.request_payload->>'stage'=%s",
+            [operation_id, command.generator_operation_name],
+        )
+        effects = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if (
+            len(effects) != 1
+            or effects[0][2]
+            .get("production_execution", {})
+            .get("process_evidence", {})
+            .get("failure_code")
+            != "OUTPUT_SCHEMA_FAILURE"
+        ):
+            return None
+        effect_id, payload, _ = effects[0]
+        runtime = payload["_production_execution"]["capability_runtime"]
+        template = verify_template_provenance(uow.cursor, runtime["routing_decision"])
+        if (
+            not template
+            or template["mode"] != "CREATIVE_VALIDATION"
+            or template["require_literary_qualification"]
+        ):
+            return None
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash FROM authority_operation_output_asset output JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) WHERE output.operation_id=%s AND a.asset_type='model_call_log' AND v.content->>'stage'=%s",
+            [operation_id, command.generator_operation_name],
+        )
+        logs = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if len(logs) != 1:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_SOURCE_INVALID",
+                "Require the one original native model-call record",
+            )
+    retained = read_rejected_structured_completion(
+        application.database_url,
+        EffectCommandContext(
+            operation_id=operation_id,
+            project_id=command.project_id,
+            actor_id=command.actor_id,
+            trace_id=command.trace_id,
+            correlation_id=command.correlation_id,
+        ),
+        effect_id=effect_id,
+    )
+    provider = retained["provider_evidence"]
+    production = provider["production_execution"]
+    request = production["execution_request"]
+    if (
+        request["external_effect_id"] != effect_id
+        or request["operation_id"] != operation_id
+        or request["project_id"] != command.project_id
+        or request["book_id"] != command.book_id
+        or request["stage"] != command.generator_operation_name
+        or any(
+            runtime[key] != provider["capability_runtime"][key]
+            for key in (
+                "materialization_hash",
+                "provider_request_envelope_hash",
+                "provider_admission_asset_version_id",
+            )
+        )
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_SCOPE_DRIFT",
+            "Retain the exact dispatched scope and runtime",
+        )
+    query = AuthorityAssetQuery(application.database_url)
+    model_log = query.require(
+        project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=logs[0][0]
+    )
+    if model_log.content_hash != logs[0][1] or any(
+        model_log.content.get(key) != value for key, value in provider.items()
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_EVIDENCE_DRIFT",
+            "Retain the immutable owned model-call evidence for the failed dispatch",
+        )
+    raw, null_paths, process_doc, raw_doc = retained_output(
+        query, project_id=command.project_id, provider=provider, request=request
+    )
+    from .result_recovery import validate_raw_result
+
+    validate_raw_result(
+        command.generator_operation_name,
+        raw,
+        work_requirements=command.execution_contract_version in {"1.17.0", "1.18.0"},
+        aligned_methods=command.execution_contract_version == "1.18.0",
+    )
+    snapshot = CapabilityPackRuntimeResolver(
+        application.database_url
+    ).require_attempt_snapshot(
+        attempt_id=request["attempt_id"],
+        snapshot_id=runtime["snapshot_id"],
+        snapshot_hash=runtime["snapshot_hash"],
+    )
+    if (
+        snapshot.operation_id != operation_id
+        or snapshot.execution_fencing_token != request["fencing_token"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_SNAPSHOT_DRIFT",
+            "Retain the exact original fenced Pack snapshot",
+        )
+    frozen = snapshot.execution_snapshot
+    context_ref = production["input_manifest"]["context_bundle_version"]
+    context = query.require(project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=context_ref)
+    artifact = next(
+        a
+        for a in production["input_manifest"]["input_artifacts"]
+        if a["asset_version_id"] == context_ref
+    )
+    if context.content_hash != artifact["content_hash"]:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_CONTEXT_DRIFT",
+            "Retain the exact dispatched Context",
+        )
+    inputs = tuple(FrozenPlanningInput(**i) for i in source[2])
+    documents = [
+        query.require(
+            project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=i.asset_version_id
+        )
+        for i in inputs
+    ]
+    if any(
+        d.content_hash != i.content_hash or d.asset_type != i.asset_type
+        for i, d in zip(inputs, documents, strict=True)
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_INPUT_DRIFT",
+            "Retain every original input and role",
+        )
+    selection_hash = context.content["narrative_intelligence"]["selection_digest"]
+    binding = PlanningCapabilityBinding(
+        binding_id=_identity("planning-capability-binding", request["attempt_id"]),
+        operation_id=operation_id,
+        attempt_id=request["attempt_id"],
+        fencing_token=request["fencing_token"],
+        project_id=command.project_id,
+        workspace_id=command.workspace_id,
+        book_id=command.book_id,
+        capability_ref=frozen.capability_ref,
+        capability_hash=frozen.capability_hash,
+        execution_capability_id=frozen.execution_capability_id,
+        execution_capability_hash=frozen.execution_capability_hash,
+        input_manifest_ref=request["input_manifest_ref"],
+        context_bundle_asset_version_id=context_ref,
+        context_bundle_hash=context.content_hash,
+        narrative_intelligence_selection_digest=selection_hash,
+        commit_authority=frozen.commit_authority,
+        capability_pack_attempt_snapshot_id=snapshot.snapshot_id,
+        capability_pack_attempt_snapshot_hash=snapshot.snapshot_hash,
+    )
+    recovery_id = "planning-gateway-revalidation:" + operation_id
+    provenance = {
+        "contract_version": VERSION,
+        "source_operation_id": operation_id,
+        "external_effect_id": effect_id,
+        "recovery_id": recovery_id,
+        "source_result_asset_version_id": logs[0][0],
+        "source_result_hash": canonical_hash(raw),
+        "source_release_sha": frozen.release_sha,
+        "target_release_sha": application.commit_sha,
+        "operator_id": principal.principal_id,
+        "source_pack_snapshot_id": snapshot.snapshot_id,
+        "source_pack_snapshot_hash": snapshot.snapshot_hash,
+        "source_context_asset_version_id": context_ref,
+        "source_context_hash": context.content_hash,
+        "original_failure": "OUTPUT_SCHEMA_FAILURE",
+        "original_gateway_outcome": "FINAL_FAILURE",
+        "current_result_validation": "VALID",
+        "legacy_literal_null_paths": null_paths,
+        "raw_output_receipt": {
+            "asset_version_id": raw_doc.asset_version_id,
+            "content_hash": raw_doc.content_hash,
+        },
+        "process_receipt": {
+            "asset_version_id": process_doc.asset_version_id,
+            "content_hash": process_doc.content_hash,
+        },
+        "provider_invoked": False,
+        "literary_qualification": "NOT_RUN",
+        "reason": "Correct known literal-null Schema engine rejection without resending the completed process",
+    }
+    from .result_admission import definition_for
+
+    definition = definition_for(application)
+    definition.update(
+        name=NAME,
+        handler_key=HANDLER,
+        handler_contract_version=VERSION,
+        policy={
+            "execution_classification": "transaction_command",
+            "source_actor_or_scoped_operator_required": True,
+            "provider_dispatch_allowed": False,
+        },
+        idempotency_scope="book:planning-gateway-revalidation",
+        concurrency_key_template="book:{book_id}:planning-gateway-revalidation",
+        completion_contract={
+            **definition["completion_contract"],
+            "required_outbox_events": [NAME],
+        },
+    )
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.repositories.definitions.register(definition)
+        uow.commit()
+
+    def materialize(uow, registration_id, checkpoint):
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT status FROM authority_operation WHERE operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [operation_id],
+        )
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone() != ("FAILED_FINAL",):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_OWNER_DRIFT",
+                "Retain the failed-final original owner",
+            )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT candidate_asset_version_id,version FROM authority_story_planning_generation WHERE operation_id=%s FOR UPDATE",
+            [operation_id],
+        )
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone() != (None, source[3]):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_STALE",
+                "Source generation changed before atomic result admission",
+            )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s AND status NOT IN ('SUCCEEDED','FAILED_FINAL')) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_budget_reservation WHERE operation_id=%s AND status<>'SETTLED') OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id) WHERE a.operation_id=%s AND l.status='HELD')",
+            [operation_id] * 3,
+        )
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+            raise KernelError(
+                "PLANNING_GATEWAY_RESULT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
+                "No unresolved source call or usage may enter recovery",
+            )
+        validation = uow.repositories.asset_documents.materialize_output(
+            AuthorityAssetWriteCommand(
+                operation_id=registration_id,
+                project_id=command.project_id,
+                asset_id=recovery_id + ":validation",
+                asset_type="planning_gateway_result_revalidation",
+                schema_version=VERSION,
+                logical_ref=f"planning/gateway_revalidation/{operation_id}/validation",
+                category="runs",
+                output_key="story_planning.gateway_revalidation",
+                role="local_validation",
+                content={"evidence": provenance, "raw_result": raw},
+                qualification_policy="planning-local-schema-validation-not-certified-v1",
+                trace_id=command.trace_id,
+                correlation_id=command.correlation_id,
+            )
+        )
+        reference = "authority-asset-version:" + validation.asset_version_id
+        pep = PlanningExecutionEvidence(
+            operation_id=operation_id,
+            attempt_id=request["attempt_id"],
+            fencing_token=request["fencing_token"],
+            route_snapshot_id=request["route_snapshot_id"],
+            input_manifest_ref=request["input_manifest_ref"],
+            output_manifest_ref=reference,
+            validation_manifest_ref=reference,
+            usage_receipt_ref=production["execution_result"]["usage_receipt_ref"],
+            cost_receipt_ref=production["execution_result"]["cost_receipt_ref"],
+            external_receipt_ref=effect_id,

+def require_capacity_source(uow, *, source_operation_id, project_id, frozen, version, routing_failure=False, input_order_failure=False):
+    from .application import _command_from_payload
+    from .result_recovery import require_result_repair_source
+
+    command = _command_from_payload(frozen)
+    if not routing_failure and not input_order_failure and (not command.repair_of_operation_id or not command.repair_result_asset_version_id):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED", "Capacity recovery requires a frozen raw-result repair")
+    uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT
+        (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s),
+        (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_budget_reservation WHERE operation_id=%s),
+        (SELECT count(*) FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=%s AND status IN ('CLAIMED','EXECUTING','WAITING_EXTERNAL_RESULT'))""",
+        [source_operation_id]*3)
+    if any(uow.cursor.fetchone()):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_DISPATCH_UNSAFE", "Capacity successor requires no Effect, reservation or live Attempt")
+    if input_order_failure:
+        retained, blocked_decision = require_input_order_source(uow,command=command,source_operation_id=source_operation_id)
+    elif routing_failure:
+        retained, blocked_decision = require_route_source(uow,command=command,source_operation_id=source_operation_id)
+    else:
+        retained = require_result_repair_source(uow.database_url, command=command, operation_id=command.repair_of_operation_id,
+            source_asset_version_id=command.repair_result_asset_version_id)
+    result_operation_id = retained.get("operation_id",command.repair_of_operation_id)
+    effect_id = retained["execution_evidence"]["external_receipt_ref"]
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT provider,model_id,request_payload FROM authority_external_effect WHERE external_effect_id=%s AND operation_id=%s AND status='SUCCEEDED'", [effect_id,result_operation_id])
+    effect = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not effect:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_EVIDENCE_MISSING", "Original result must retain its settled successful Provider call")
+    capability = effect[2]["_production_execution"]["capability_runtime"]
+    policy = verify_template_provenance(uow.cursor, capability["routing_decision"])
+    if not policy or policy["mode"] != "CREATIVE_VALIDATION" or policy["require_literary_qualification"] or policy["book_id"] != command.book_id:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_POLICY_REQUIRED", "Capacity recovery cannot relax strict production")
+    evidence_id = capability["provider_admission_asset_version_id"]
+    uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT v.content_hash,v.content,a.asset_type FROM authority_operation_output_asset o
+        JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) JOIN authority_asset a ON a.asset_id=v.asset_id
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.asset_version_id=%s""", [result_operation_id,evidence_id])
+    receipt = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not receipt or receipt[0] != capability["provider_admission_content_hash"] or receipt[2] != "provider_request_admission_manifest":
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_EVIDENCE_INVALID", "Original native Provider admission identity is missing")
+    identity = receipt[1].get("contract",receipt[1])["provider_request_identity"]
+    if identity["model"] != effect[1]:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_MODEL_ROUTE_DRIFT", "Original result model identity differs")
+    uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT v.content->'contract' FROM authority_operation_output_asset o
+        JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) JOIN authority_asset a ON a.asset_id=v.asset_id
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND a.asset_type='route_snapshot'
+            AND v.content #>> '{contract,route_snapshot_id}'=%s""",
+        [result_operation_id, retained["execution_evidence"]["route_snapshot_id"]])
+    route = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not route or not route[0].get("auth_profile_ref"):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_ROUTE_EVIDENCE_REQUIRED", "Original result must retain its exact native authentication binding")
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT snapshot_payload FROM authority_attempt_capability_pack_snapshot WHERE operation_id=%s ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1", [source_operation_id])
+    row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT snapshot_payload FROM authority_attempt_capability_pack_snapshot WHERE snapshot_id=%s AND snapshot_hash=%s AND operation_id=%s",
+        [capability["snapshot_id"],capability["snapshot_hash"],result_operation_id])
+    parent_snapshot = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not row or not parent_snapshot or row[0]["route_policy_hash"] != parent_snapshot[0]["route_policy_hash"]:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_SNAPSHOT_MISSING", "Capacity source must retain the original route and failed Pack snapshot")
+    snapshot = row[0]
+    if ("planning_task_token_budget" not in json.loads(snapshot["context_policy_payload_json"])
+            and (snapshot["context_policy_hash"] != parent_snapshot[0]["context_policy_hash"]
+                 or snapshot["catalog_snapshot_hash"] != parent_snapshot[0]["catalog_snapshot_hash"])):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_CAPACITY_DRIFT", "Legacy capacity must retain the original result's exact frozen Context policy and catalog")
+    depth = 0

+        prior = uow.cursor.fetchone()

+        depth = prior[0]

+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT attempt_id,result,error FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=%s ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1", [source_operation_id])
+    attempt = uow.cursor.fetchone()

+        "source_reasoning_effort":route[0]["reasoning_effort"],
+        "preflight_failure":{"contract_version":"planning-input-order-preflight.v1" if input_order_failure else "planning-repair-route-preflight.v1" if routing_failure else "planning-capacity-preflight.v1",
+            **({"blocked_decision":blocked_decision} if routing_failure or input_order_failure else {}),"attempt_id":attempt[0],
+            "result":attempt[1],"error":attempt[2],"effect_count":0,"reservation_count":0,
+            "repair_number":command.repair_number,"source_result_asset_version_id":retained["asset_version_id"],
+            "source_result_hash":retained["content_hash"],"settled_original_effect_id":effect_id}}
+
