+def require_capacity_target(source, target):
+    old = json.loads(source["snapshot"]["context_policy_payload_json"])
+    new = json.loads(target["pack_snapshot"]["context_policy_payload_json"])
+    if source["preflight_failure"]["contract_version"] in {"planning-repair-route-preflight.v1","planning-input-order-preflight.v1"}:
+        if old != new:
+            raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_CAPACITY_DRIFT","Routing recovery retains the exact Context policy")
+        return {"context_policy":"UNCHANGED","recovery_kind":"NATIVE_BOUNDED_REPAIR_ROUTE","book_usage_budget":False}
+    original = old.pop("planning_task_token_budget", source["original_context_target"])
+    successor = new.pop("planning_task_token_budget",None)
+    if old != new or type(original) is not int or type(successor) is not int or not original < successor <= old["max_tokens"] <= 512000:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_CAPACITY_DRIFT", "Only the bounded Planning task Context target may increase")
+    return {"original_target":original,"successor_target":successor,
+        "system_ceiling":old["max_tokens"],"unchanged_context_policy_hash":canonical_hash(old),
+        "book_usage_budget":False}
+
+
+
+def require_route_source(uow, *, command, source_operation_id):
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing import native_planning_repair
+    from .execution import _planning_repair_routing_lineage
+    lineage = _planning_repair_routing_lineage(uow.database_url,project_id=command.project_id,book_id=command.book_id,
+        generator_operation_name=command.generator_operation_name,operation_id=source_operation_id,
+        repair_of_operation_id=command.repair_of_operation_id,repair_of_candidate_id=command.repair_of_candidate_id)
+    evidence = native_planning_repair(uow.cursor,project_id=command.project_id,book_id=command.book_id,
+        operation_id=source_operation_id,task_type=command.generator_operation_name,lineage=lineage)
+    if not evidence:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED","Only an actual creative native repair may receive a routing successor")
+    uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT v.content,v.content_hash,v.asset_version_id FROM authority_operation_output_asset o
+        JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) JOIN authority_asset a ON a.asset_id=v.asset_id
+        WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND a.asset_type='routing_decision'""",[source_operation_id])
+    decisions = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+    if len(decisions) != 1:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_EVIDENCE_INVALID","Blocked repair requires its exact routing decision")
+    decision, digest, decision_id = decisions[0]
+    template = verify_template_provenance(uow.cursor,decision)
+    candidate = evidence["parent_candidate_id"]
+    rows = [row for row in decision["candidates"] if row["candidate"]["candidate_id"] == candidate]
+    if (not template or template['mode'] != 'CREATIVE_VALIDATION' or template['require_literary_qualification']
+            or decision['status'] != 'BLOCKED' or len(rows) != 1
+            or set(rows[0]['rejected_reasons']) - {'FAILED_ROUTE_EXCLUDED','SEMANTIC_ARBITRATION_REQUIRED','ROUTE_RETRY_LIMIT_REACHED'}
+            or 'FAILED_ROUTE_EXCLUDED' not in rows[0]['rejected_reasons']
+            or not decision['task']['history']
+            or any(h.get('failure_class') not in {'SCHEMA_REJECTION','MALFORMED_STRUCTURED_OUTPUT',''}
+                for h in decision['task']['history'])):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED","Only bounded contract-repair failed-route exclusion permits this successor")
+    blocked = {'asset_version_id':decision_id,'content_hash':digest,
+        'decision':decision,'native_repair_evidence':evidence}
+    if command.repair_result_asset_version_id:
+        from .result_recovery import require_result_repair_source
+        retained = require_result_repair_source(uow.database_url,command=command,
+            operation_id=evidence['parent_operation_id'],source_asset_version_id=evidence['source_result_asset_version_id'])
+        return {**retained,'operation_id':evidence['parent_operation_id']}, blocked
+    return retained_candidate_source(uow,evidence=evidence), blocked
+
+
+def retained_candidate_source(uow, *, evidence):
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT content FROM authority_asset_version WHERE asset_version_id=%s AND content_hash=%s",
+        [evidence['source_result_asset_version_id'],evidence['source_result_hash']])
+    content = uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]
+    execution = content['execution_evidence']
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT v.content FROM authority_operation_output_asset o JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id) WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND v.asset_version_id=%s",
+        [evidence['parent_operation_id'],execution['context_bundle_asset_version_id']])
+    context = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not context or canonical_hash(context[0]) != execution['context_bundle_hash']:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_EVIDENCE_INVALID","Original native repair Context is missing or changed")
+    return {'operation_id':evidence['parent_operation_id'],'execution_evidence':execution,
+        'context_content':context[0],'asset_version_id':evidence['source_result_asset_version_id'],
+        'content_hash':evidence['source_result_hash']}
+

+def require_input_order_source(uow, *, command, source_operation_id):
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing import native_planning_repair
+    from .execution import _planning_repair_routing_lineage
+    if not command.repair_of_candidate_id or command.repair_of_operation_id:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED","Input-order recovery requires an exact native candidate repair")
+    lineage = _planning_repair_routing_lineage(uow.database_url,project_id=command.project_id,book_id=command.book_id,
+        generator_operation_name=command.generator_operation_name,operation_id=source_operation_id,
+        repair_of_operation_id=command.repair_of_operation_id,repair_of_candidate_id=command.repair_of_candidate_id)
+    evidence = native_planning_repair(uow.cursor,project_id=command.project_id,book_id=command.book_id,
+        operation_id=source_operation_id,task_type=command.generator_operation_name,lineage=lineage)
+    if not evidence or evidence['input_snapshot_hash'] == evidence['parent_input_snapshot_hash']:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED","Only an identical role map with changed ordered input hash permits this successor")
+    uow.cursor.execute("SELECT report_hash FROM authority_story_planning_validation_report WHERE candidate_asset_version_id=%s AND status='FAIL'",[command.repair_of_candidate_id])
+    if not any(command.repair_reason.endswith(row[0]) for row in uow.cursor.fetchall()):
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_EVIDENCE_INVALID","Repair must retain its native rejected candidate report")
+    return retained_candidate_source(uow,evidence=evidence), {'native_repair_evidence':evidence,
+        'source_operation_id':source_operation_id,'failure_code':'NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID'}

+NAME = "story_planning.result_admission.recover"
+HANDLER = "novel_forge." + NAME

+def recover(application, *, source_operation_id, project_id, source_asset_version_id, reason, principal):
+    from .application import _command_from_payload
+
+    principal.require("story_planning.authority.execute", project_id)
+    if principal.principal_type is not PrincipalType.LOCAL_OPERATOR or not str(reason).strip():
+        raise PermissionError("Result admission recovery requires a trusted operator and an explicit reason")
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT r.recovery_id,r.recovery_operation_id,r.candidate_asset_version_id,v.content_hash,r.evidence,o.status
+            FROM authority_planning_result_admission r JOIN authority_asset_version v ON v.asset_version_id=r.candidate_asset_version_id
+            JOIN authority_operation o ON o.operation_id=r.recovery_operation_id
+            WHERE r.source_operation_id=%s AND r.project_id=%s""", [source_operation_id,project_id])
+        existing = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if existing:
+            if (existing[5] != "SUCCEEDED" or existing[4]["source_result_asset_version_id"] != source_asset_version_id
+                    or existing[4]["reason"] != reason or existing[4]["operator_id"] != principal.principal_id
+                    or existing[4]["target_release_sha"] != application.commit_sha):
+                raise KernelError("PLANNING_RESULT_ADMISSION_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT", "Existing recovery belongs to a different frozen decision")
+            return {"recovery_id":existing[0],"operation_id":existing[1],"asset_version_id":existing[2],

+                or runtime["routing_decision"]["task"]["operation_id"] != source_operation_id):
+            raise KernelError("PLANNING_RESULT_ADMISSION_POLICY_REQUIRED", "Result processing cannot confer strict production qualification")
+    snapshot = CapabilityPackRuntimeResolver(application.database_url).require_attempt_snapshot(
+        attempt_id=evidence["attempt_id"], snapshot_id=runtime["snapshot_id"], snapshot_hash=runtime["snapshot_hash"])

+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RESULT_ADMISSION_SNAPSHOT_DRIFT", source_operation_id)

+    selection_digest = context["narrative_intelligence"]["selection_digest"]
+    binding = PlanningCapabilityBinding(binding_id=_identity("planning-capability-binding", evidence["attempt_id"]),
+        operation_id=source_operation_id, attempt_id=evidence["attempt_id"], fencing_token=evidence["fencing_token"],
+        project_id=project_id, workspace_id=command.workspace_id, book_id=command.book_id,
+        capability_ref=frozen.capability_ref, capability_hash=frozen.capability_hash,
+        execution_capability_id=frozen.execution_capability_id, execution_capability_hash=frozen.execution_capability_hash,

+        context_bundle_hash=retained["context_hash"], narrative_intelligence_selection_digest=selection_digest,

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from dataclasses import replace
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import (
+    decision_template,
+    selected_book_template,
+    verify_template_provenance,

+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork

+def repair_stage(payload):
+    from .world_result_recovery import WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS
+
+    if (payload.get("repair_result_diagnostic") or {}).get(
+        "error_code"
+    ) in WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS:
+        return "world_candidates.generate"
+    return payload["generator_operation_name"]
+
+
+def stage_ordinal(cursor, *, lineage, stage):
+    cursor.execute(
+        "SELECT g.operation_id,g.repair_number,o.command_payload->'payload',e.external_effect_id,e.status,r.status,e.request_payload->>'stage' FROM authority_story_planning_generation g JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id) LEFT JOIN authority_external_effect e USING(operation_id) LEFT JOIN authority_budget_reservation r USING(external_effect_id) WHERE g.operation_id=ANY(%s::text[])",
+        [list(lineage)],
+    )
+    rows = cursor.fetchall()
+    if {row[0] for row in rows} != set(lineage) or any(
+        row[3] and (row[4] not in {"SUCCEEDED", "FAILED_FINAL"} or row[5] != "SETTLED")
+        for row in rows
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "PLANNING_STAGE_REPAIR_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
+            "Preserve every original call and settled usage before phase repair",
+        )
+    return len(
+        {
+            row[1]
+            for row in rows
+            if row[1] and row[3] and row[6] == stage and repair_stage(row[2]) == stage
+        }
+    )
+
+
+def repair_bound(application, *, command, operation_id, maximum):
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        admission = selected_book_template(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=command.project_id, book_id=command.book_id
+        )
+        template = (
+            decision_template(
+                {
+                    "task": {
+                        "work_id": command.book_id,
+                        "policy_snapshot": admission["policy_snapshot"],
+                    }
+                }
+            )
+            if admission
+            else None
+        )
+        if not template or template["version"] not in {"creative-execution-template.v4", "creative-execution-template.v5","creative-execution-template.v6"}:
+            return None
+        from .execution import _planning_repair_routing_lineage
+
+        lineage = _planning_repair_routing_lineage(
+            application.database_url,
+            project_id=command.project_id,
+            book_id=command.book_id,
+            generator_operation_name=command.generator_operation_name,
+            operation_id=operation_id,
+            repair_of_operation_id=command.repair_of_operation_id,
+            repair_of_candidate_id=command.repair_of_candidate_id,
+        )
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT request_payload #> '{_production_execution,capability_runtime,routing_decision}' FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s AND request_payload->>'stage'=%s",
+            [operation_id, command.generator_operation_name],
+        )
+        decisions = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        stage = command.generator_operation_name
+        if not decisions and command.world_preparation_request:
+            stage = "world_candidates.generate"
+            uow.cursor.execute(
+                "SELECT request_payload #> '{_production_execution,capability_runtime,routing_decision}' FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s AND request_payload->>'stage'=%s",
+                [operation_id, stage],
+            )
+            decisions = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if not decisions:
+            return None
+        if len(decisions) != 1:
+            raise KernelError("PLANNING_STAGE_REPAIR_SOURCE_AMBIGUOUS", operation_id)
+        source = verify_template_provenance(uow.cursor, decisions[0][0])
+        if (
+            not source
+            or source["mode"] != "CREATIVE_VALIDATION"
+            or source["require_literary_qualification"]
+            or source["member_authorization_ref"]
+            != template["member_authorization_ref"]
+            or source["require_independent_review"]
+            != template["require_independent_review"]
+        ):
+            return None
+        ordinal = stage_ordinal(uow.cursor, lineage=lineage, stage=stage)
+    return {
+        "version": "native-stage-repair-bound.v1",
+        "stage": stage,
+        "stage_repair_number": ordinal,
+        "monotonic_repair_number": command.repair_number,
+        "maximum_repairs": min(
+            maximum, template["repair_routing"]["max_same_route_repairs"]
+        ),
+        "lineage_operation_ids": list(lineage),
+        "policy_admission": admission,
+    }
+
+
+def follow_rejected(
+    application, *, command, operation_id, candidate_asset_version_id, principal
+):
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT status FROM authority_story_planning_generation WHERE operation_id=%s AND candidate_asset_version_id=%s",
+            [operation_id, candidate_asset_version_id],
+        )
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone() not in {("REJECTED",), ("REPAIR_REQUIRED",)}:
+            return None
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT report,report_hash FROM authority_story_planning_validation_report WHERE candidate_asset_version_id=%s AND status='FAIL' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1",
+            [candidate_asset_version_id],
+        )
+        failure = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not failure:
+        return None
+    from .application import GENERATOR_CATALOG
+
+    definition = GENERATOR_CATALOG[command.generator_operation_name]
+    maximum = (
+        definition.maximum_semantic_repairs
+        if failure[0]["validation_kind"] == "SEMANTIC"
+        else definition.maximum_structural_regenerations
+    )
+    bound = repair_bound(
+        application, command=command, operation_id=operation_id, maximum=maximum
+    )
+    if bound is None or bound["stage_repair_number"] >= bound["maximum_repairs"]:
+        return None
+    repaired = replace(
+        command,
+        runtime_continuation_id="",
+        repair_of_candidate_id=candidate_asset_version_id,
+        repair_of_operation_id="",
+        repair_result_asset_version_id="",
+        repair_result_diagnostic=None,
+        repair_reason=f"{failure[0]['error_code']}:{failure[1]}",
+        repair_number=command.repair_number + 1,
+        idempotency_key=f"{command.idempotency_key}:repair:{command.repair_number + 1}",
+    )
+    return application.generate(repaired, principal=principal)
+
+
+def resume_rejected_book_plan(composition, book, *, worker):
+    if book["state"] != "PLAN_APPROVAL_PENDING":
+        return False
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(composition.authority.database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT g.operation_id,o.command_payload->'payload',g.candidate_asset_version_id FROM authority_story_planning_generation g JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id) WHERE g.project_id=%s AND g.book_id=%s AND g.logical_key=%s AND g.status IN ('REJECTED','REPAIR_REQUIRED') ORDER BY g.created_at DESC LIMIT 1",
+            [
+                book["project_id"],
+                book["book_id"],
+                f"chapter:{book['accepted_through_chapter'] + 1}",
+            ],
+        )
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    if not row:
+        return False
+    from .application import _command_from_payload, GENERATOR_CATALOG
+
+    command = _command_from_payload(row[1])
+    worker.require("book.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    composition.principal.require("story_planning.authority.execute", book["project_id"])
+    if (
+        command.actor_id != composition.principal.principal_id
+        or (command.world_preparation_request or {}).get("book_run_id")
+        != book["book_run_id"]
+        or (command.world_preparation_request or {}).get("accepted_through_chapter")
+        != book["accepted_through_chapter"]
+        or not command.auto_approve_low_risk
+    ):
+        return False
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_design_authority.creative_validation import (
+        candidate_validation,
+    )
+
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(composition.authority.database_url) as uow:
+        provenance = candidate_validation(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=book["project_id"], asset_version_id=row[2]
+        )
+    if (
+        not provenance
+        or provenance["generation_operation_id"] != row[0]
+        or provenance["book_id"] != book["book_id"]
+    ):
+        return False
+    application = composition.formation.generation.planning
+    bound = repair_bound(
+        application,
+        command=command,
+        operation_id=row[0],
+        maximum=GENERATOR_CATALOG[
+            command.generator_operation_name
+        ].maximum_structural_regenerations,
+    )
+    if bound is None or bound["stage_repair_number"] >= bound["maximum_repairs"]:
+        return False
+    from novel_forge.modules.book_authority.production import BookProductionState
+
+    composition.authority.advance_supervisor_checkpoint(
+        book["book_run_id"],
+        expected_version=book["version"],
+        expected_state=book["state"],
+        target_state=BookProductionState.CHAPTER_PLAN_REQUIRED,
+        principal=worker,
+    )
+    return True

+from __future__ import annotations

+def optional_policy(cursor, *, project_id, book_id, operation_id=None):
+    admission = selected_book_template(cursor, project_id=project_id, book_id=book_id)
+    value = (
+        decision_template(
+            {
+                "task": {
+                    "work_id": book_id,
+                    "policy_snapshot": admission["policy_snapshot"],
+                }
+            }
+        )
+        if admission
+        else None
+    )
+    if (
+        not value
+        or value["version"] not in {"creative-execution-template.v3","creative-execution-template.v4","creative-execution-template.v5","creative-execution-template.v6"}
+        or value["world_future_preparation"] == "REQUIRED"
+    ):
+        return None
+    if operation_id:
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT v.policy_snapshot,o.status FROM authority_attempt_capability_pack_snapshot s JOIN authority_capability_pack_validation_admission v ON v.compilation_id=s.snapshot_payload->>'compilation_id' AND v.project_id=%s AND v.book_id=%s JOIN authority_operation o ON o.operation_id=v.operation_id WHERE s.operation_id=%s ORDER BY s.created_at DESC LIMIT 1",
+            [project_id, book_id, operation_id],
+        )
+        frozen = cursor.fetchone()
+        if (
+            not frozen
+            or frozen[1] != "SUCCEEDED"
+            or frozen[0] != admission["policy_snapshot"]
+        ):
+            return None
+    return admission
+
+
+def require_optional_source(source):
+    if source.get("actor_experiment") or source.get("preparation_sources"):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_DEPENDENCY_REQUIRED",
+            "Independent actor experiments and accepted preparation commitments cannot be skipped",
+        )
+
+
+def deferred_action(*, source, source_ref, command, admission, rejected=None):
+    require_optional_source(source)
+    plan = (
+        rejected["world_plan"]
+        if rejected
+        else {
+            "contract_version": "world-production-plan.v1",
+            "authority": "PLANNING_PROPOSAL",
+            "baseline_id": source["reality"]["revision_id"],
+            "accepted_through_chapter": source["reality"]["chapter_number"],
+            "chapter_number": int(source["request"]["accepted_through_chapter"]) + 1,
+            "intent_version_id": source["intent_version_id"],
+            "policy": source["policy"],
+            "selected_candidate_id": "",
+            "selected_path_id": "",
+            "causal_debts": [],
+            "candidate_futures": [],
+            "preparation_schedule": [],
+            "alternative_paths": [],
+            "rejected_paths": [],
+            "continuation_decisions": [],
+            "fact_mutation_allowed": False,
+        }
+    )
+    if rejected and (
+        plan["disposition"] != "BLOCKED"
+        or any(c.get("eligible") for c in plan["candidate_futures"])
+        or plan["selected_candidate_id"]
+        or plan["preparation_schedule"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_RESULT_INVALID",
+            "Only rejected unselected speculative futures may be deferred",
+        )
+    reason = "Optional world future simulation deferred by the trusted execution template; no simulated event becomes a fact."
+    return {
+        "contract_version": "world-production-action.v2",
+        "authority": "PLANNING_PROPOSAL",
+        "project_id": command.project_id,
+        "book_id": command.book_id,
+        "book_run_id": source["request"]["book_run_id"],
+        "actor_id": command.actor_id,
+        "source": source_ref,
+        "generation": None,
+        "evaluation": None,
+        "world_plan": _seal(
+            {
+                **{key: value for key, value in plan.items() if key != "plan_digest"},
+                "disposition": "DEFER",
+                "reason": reason,
+            }
+        ),
+        "preserve_accepted_history": True,
+        "fact_mutation_allowed": False,
+        "optional_preparation": {
+            "contract_version": "world-optional-preparation.v1",
+            "status": "NOT_RUN",
+            "reason": "REJECTED_OPTIONAL_SEARCH_DEFERRED"
+            if rejected
+            else "DISABLED_BY_TEMPLATE",
+            "policy_admission": admission,
+            "prior_simulation_status": "REJECTED" if rejected else "NOT_RUN",
+        },
+    }
+
+
+def validate_optional_action(cursor, *, action, source):
+    marker = action["optional_preparation"]
+    admission = marker["policy_admission"]
+    template = verify_template_provenance(
+        cursor,
+        {
+            "task": {
+                "project_id": action["project_id"],
+                "work_id": action["book_id"],
+                "policy_snapshot": admission["policy_snapshot"],
+            }
+        },
+    )
+    if (
+        not template
+        or template["version"] not in {"creative-execution-template.v3","creative-execution-template.v4","creative-execution-template.v5","creative-execution-template.v6"}
+        or template["mode"] != "CREATIVE_VALIDATION"
+        or template["world_future_preparation"] == "REQUIRED"
+        or marker["contract_version"] != "world-optional-preparation.v1"
+        or marker["status"] != "NOT_RUN"
+        or action.get("generation") is not None
+        or action.get("evaluation") is not None
+        or action["world_plan"]["disposition"] != "DEFER"
+        or action["world_plan"]["selected_candidate_id"]
+        or action["world_plan"]["preparation_schedule"]
+        or any(c.get("eligible") for c in action["world_plan"]["candidate_futures"])
+        or action["world_plan"].get("fact_mutation_allowed") is not False
+        or action.get("actor_proposals")
+        or action.get("fact_mutation_allowed") is not False
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_POLICY_INVALID",
+            "Deferral cannot certify a future, waive strict policy or mutate facts",
+        )
+    require_optional_source(source)
+    retained = marker.get("retained_rejected_action")
+    if retained:
+        cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_asset_version v JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) WHERE v.asset_version_id=%s AND a.project_id=%s AND a.asset_type='narrative_governance_action'",
+            [retained["asset_version_id"], action["project_id"]],
+        )
+        row = cursor.fetchone()
+        if (
+            not row
+            or row[0] != retained["content_hash"]
+            or row[0] != canonical_hash(row[1])
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_RESULT_INVALID",
+                "Retained rejection requires its exact original action",
+            )
+        old = row[1]["contract"]
+        if (
+            old["source"] != action["source"]
+            or old["world_plan"]["disposition"] != "BLOCKED"
+        ):
+            raise KernelError(
+                "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_RESULT_INVALID",
+                "Deferral must preserve its rejected world source",
+            )
+        for key in (
+            "candidate_futures",
+            "rejected_paths",
+            "baseline_id",
+            "accepted_through_chapter",
+        ):
+            if old["world_plan"][key] != action["world_plan"][key]:
+                raise KernelError(
+                    "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_RESULT_INVALID",
+                    "Deferral cannot rewrite negative or accepted evidence",
+                )
+    elif (
+        template["world_future_preparation"] != "DISABLED"
+        or action["world_plan"]["candidate_futures"]
+    ):
+        raise KernelError(
+            "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_RESULT_INVALID",
+            "Optional simulation requires its retained result; disabled simulation contributes no futures",
+        )
+
+
+def continuation_action(database_url, command):
+    if not command.runtime_continuation_id:
+        return None
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT r.decision_evidence,o.status FROM authority_story_planning_runtime_continuation r JOIN authority_operation o ON o.operation_id=r.registered_by_operation_id WHERE r.continuation_id=%s AND r.project_id=%s AND r.book_id=%s",
+            [command.runtime_continuation_id, command.project_id, command.book_id],
+        )
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+    reference = (row[0] or {}).get("world_optional_action") if row else None
+    if not reference:
+        return None
+    document = AuthorityAssetQuery(database_url).require(
+        project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=reference["asset_version_id"]
+    )
+    if row[1] != "SUCCEEDED" or document.content_hash != reference["content_hash"]:
+        raise KernelError(
+            "WORLD_OPTIONAL_DEFERRAL_REGISTRATION_INVALID",
+            "Only the completed native deferral command authorizes its successor",
+        )
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        source = AuthorityAssetQuery(database_url).require(
+            project_id=command.project_id,
+            asset_version_id=document.content["contract"]["source"]["asset_version_id"],
+        )
+        validate_optional_action(
+            uow.cursor,
+            action=document.content["contract"],
+            source=source.content["contract"],
+        )
+        WorldPreparationRepository(uow.cursor).assert_source_current(
+            project_id=command.project_id,
+            book_id=command.book_id,
+            frozen=source.content["contract"],
+        )
+    return document
+
+
+def follow_optional_failure(application, *, command, operation_id, principal):
+    from .application import _command_payload
+    from .runtime_continuation import (
+        runtime_continuation_definition,
+        resolve_target,
+        follow,
+    )
+
+    principal.require("story_planning.authority.execute", command.project_id)
+    if principal.principal_id != command.actor_id:
+        raise PermissionError(
+            "World continuation actor must be the actual execution Principal"
+        )
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(application.database_url) as uow:
+        admission = optional_policy(
+            uow.cursor, project_id=command.project_id, book_id=command.book_id
+        )
+        if admission is None:
+            return None
+        uow.cursor.execute(
+            "SELECT o.status,o.version,o.command_payload->'payload',a.error FROM authority_operation o JOIN LATERAL (SELECT result AS error FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=o.operation_id ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1) a ON TRUE WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s",
+            [operation_id, command.project_id],
+        )
+        state = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if (
+            not state
+            or state[0] not in {"FAILED_RETRYABLE", "FAILED_FINAL"}
+            or (state[3] or {}).get("error_code") != "WORLD_PREPARATION_BLOCKED"
+        ):
+            return None
+        if state[2] != _command_payload(command):
+            raise KernelError(

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from psycopg.types.json import Jsonb
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.external_effect_runtime import (
+    EffectCommandContext, read_failed_operation_success, read_checkpointed_operation_success,
+    resume_operation_from_completed_effect,
+)
+from novel_forge.modules.worldbuilding.production_planning import WorldProductionPolicy, validate_generated_candidates
+from novel_forge.modules.worldbuilding.production_schema import decode_world_generation
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_asset_store import AuthorityAssetQuery
+from novel_forge.storage.authority_uow import AuthorityUnitOfWork
+
+
+WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS = frozenset({
+    "WORLD_PROVIDER_TRANSITION_INVALID", "WORLD_GENERATION_PLAN_REFERENCE_UNKNOWN",
+    "WORLD_GENERATION_IDENTITY_UNKNOWN", "WORLD_GENERATION_BOUND_EXCEEDED",
+    "WORLD_GENERATION_DISPOSITION_INVALID", "WORLD_INFORMATION_SCOPE_INVALID",
+    "WORLD_GENERATION_IDENTITY_COLLISION", "WORLD_PREPARATION_WINDOW_INVALID",
+})
+WORLD_CHECKPOINT_ERRORS = frozenset({
+    "WORLD_GENERATION_PLAN_REFERENCE_UNKNOWN", "WORLD_GENERATION_IDENTITY_UNKNOWN",
+})
+
+
+def validate_world_generation_result(raw, source, command):
+    from .world_preparation import PlanningWorldPreparation
+    decoded = decode_world_generation({key: value for key, value in raw.items()
+        if key not in {"knowledge_translations", "actor_adjudications"}})
+    candidates = validate_generated_candidates(decoded, WorldProductionPolicy(**source["policy"]))
+    PlanningWorldPreparation._validate_references(source, candidates, command)
+    return candidates
+

+def require_world_result_repair_source(database_url, *, command, operation_id, source_asset_version_id="", require_valid_result=False, _checkpoint=False):
+    if not command.world_preparation_request:
+        return None
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT output.asset_version_id FROM authority_operation_output_asset output
+            JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+            WHERE output.operation_id=%s AND a.project_id=%s AND a.asset_type='world_preparation_generation'""",
+            [operation_id, command.project_id])
+        sources = [str(row[0]) for row in uow.cursor.fetchall()]
+    if not sources or source_asset_version_id and source_asset_version_id not in sources:
+        return None
+    if len(sources) != 1 or require_valid_result:
+        raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID", "World recovery requires one invalid generation, never a Foundation admission")
+    query = AuthorityAssetQuery(database_url)
+    document = query.require(project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=sources[0])
+    stored = document.content["contract"]
+    raw, evidence, source_ref = stored["output"], dict(stored["evidence"]), stored["source"]
+    source = query.require(project_id=command.project_id, asset_version_id=source_ref["asset_version_id"])
+    if (source.asset_type != "world_preparation_source" or source.content_hash != source_ref["content_hash"]
+            or source.content["contract"]["request"] != dict(command.world_preparation_request)
+            or evidence.get("operation_id") != operation_id):
+        raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_SOURCE_DRIFT", "World repair requires its exact Book frontier and source")
+    effect_id = str(evidence.get("external_receipt_ref") or "")
+    receipt_reader = read_checkpointed_operation_success if _checkpoint else read_failed_operation_success
+    receipt = receipt_reader(database_url, EffectCommandContext(operation_id=operation_id,
+        project_id=command.project_id, actor_id=command.actor_id, trace_id=command.trace_id,
+        correlation_id=command.correlation_id), effect_id=effect_id)
+    if receipt is None or receipt["provider_evidence"].get("result") != raw or receipt["budget_reservation_status"] != "SETTLED":
+        raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_RECEIPT_INVALID", "Reconcile exact succeeded world receipt and usage before repair")
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT request_payload FROM authority_external_effect
+            WHERE external_effect_id=%s AND operation_id=%s AND project_id=%s""", [effect_id, operation_id, command.project_id])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or row[0].get("stage") != "world_candidates.generate":
+            raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_RECEIPT_INVALID", "Repair cannot substitute another Provider stage")
+        task = row[0]["input"]["world_preparation"]
+        if task.get("source_ref") != source_ref:
+            raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_SOURCE_DRIFT", "Dispatched task must bind the retained world source")
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content FROM authority_operation_output_asset output
+            JOIN authority_asset a USING(asset_id) JOIN authority_asset_version v USING(asset_version_id)
+            WHERE output.operation_id=%s AND a.asset_type='context_bundle' AND v.content->'world_preparation'=%s::jsonb""",
+            [operation_id, Jsonb(task)])
+        contexts = uow.cursor.fetchall()
+        if len(contexts) != 1:
+            raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_CONTEXT_DRIFT", "World repair requires its exact rendered stage Context")
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s AND status<>'SUCCEEDED')
+            OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_budget_reservation WHERE operation_id=%s AND status<>'SETTLED')""", [operation_id, operation_id])
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+            raise KernelError("WORLD_RESULT_REPAIR_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED", "All original calls and usage must be resolved before a successor")
+    try:
+        validate_world_generation_result(raw, source.content["contract"], command)
+    except KernelError as error:
+        if error.code not in WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS:
+            raise
+        from .result_recovery import PlanningResultValidationError
+        diagnostic = PlanningResultValidationError(error, raw_result=raw, execution_evidence=evidence).diagnostic
+    else:
+        raise KernelError("PLANNING_RESULT_REPAIR_NOT_REQUIRED", "Valid world generation cannot authorize another generation")
+    context_id, context_hash, context = contexts[0]
+    return {"asset_version_id": document.asset_version_id, "content_hash": document.content_hash,
+        "source_operation_id": operation_id, "raw_result": raw, "diagnostic": diagnostic,
+        "execution_evidence": evidence, "schema_version": "world-result-repair.v1",
+        "context_asset_version_id": str(context_id), "context_hash": context_hash,
+        "context_content": context, "provider_stage": "world_candidates.generate", "world_source": source_ref}
+
+
+def resume_world_result_checkpoint(database_url, *, command, operation_id):
+    from .application import _command_payload
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(database_url) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT o.status,o.command_payload->'payload',g.candidate_asset_version_id,
+            a.status,a.checkpoint FROM authority_operation o
+            JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g USING(operation_id)
+            JOIN LATERAL (SELECT status,checkpoint FROM authority_attempt WHERE operation_id=o.operation_id
+                ORDER BY attempt_number DESC LIMIT 1) a ON TRUE
+            WHERE o.operation_id=%s AND o.project_id=%s AND o.definition_name='story_planning.generate'""",
+            [operation_id, command.project_id])
+        row = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        if not row or row[0] != 'WAITING_EXTERNAL_RESULT' or row[3] != 'CHECKPOINTED':
+            return False
+        code = (row[4] or {}).get('error_code')
+        if code not in WORLD_CHECKPOINT_ERRORS:
+            return False
+        if row[2] or row[1] != _command_payload(command):
+            raise KernelError('WORLD_CHECKPOINT_OWNER_DRIFT', 'Recovery requires the exact frozen Planning command without a candidate')
+        uow.cursor.execute("""SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM authority_lease_acquisition l
+            JOIN authority_attempt a USING(attempt_id) WHERE a.operation_id=%s AND l.status='HELD')""", [operation_id])
+        if uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]:
+            raise KernelError('WORLD_CHECKPOINT_LEASE_ACTIVE', 'Release the historical Attempt lease before recovery')
+    retained = require_world_result_repair_source(database_url, command=command, operation_id=operation_id, _checkpoint=True)
+    if not retained or retained['diagnostic']['error_code'] != code:
+        raise KernelError('WORLD_CHECKPOINT_DIAGNOSTIC_DRIFT', 'The retained result must reproduce its exact known identifier failure')
+    resume_operation_from_completed_effect(database_url, EffectCommandContext(operation_id=operation_id,
+        project_id=command.project_id, actor_id=command.actor_id, trace_id=command.trace_id,
+        correlation_id=command.correlation_id), effect_id=retained['execution_evidence']['external_receipt_ref'])
+    return True
+
+
+def world_result_failures(cursor, *, project_id, effect_ids):
+    if not effect_ids:
+        return {}
+    cursor.execute("""SELECT e.external_effect_id,v.asset_version_id,v.content_hash,v.content,
+        o.command_payload->'payload',s.content,s.content_hash FROM authority_external_effect e
+        JOIN authority_operation o USING(operation_id)
+        JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g USING(operation_id)
+        JOIN authority_operation_output_asset output ON output.operation_id=o.operation_id
+        JOIN authority_asset a ON a.asset_id=output.asset_id AND a.project_id=e.project_id
+        JOIN authority_asset_version v ON v.asset_version_id=output.asset_version_id
+        JOIN authority_asset_version s ON s.asset_version_id=v.content #>> '{contract,source,asset_version_id}'
+        WHERE e.project_id=%s AND e.external_effect_id=ANY(%s) AND e.status='SUCCEEDED'
+          AND o.status='FAILED_FINAL' AND g.candidate_asset_version_id IS NULL
+          AND a.asset_type='world_preparation_generation' AND v.governance='CLEAR'
+          AND v.content #>> '{contract,evidence,external_receipt_ref}'=e.external_effect_id
+          AND e.request_payload->>'stage'='world_candidates.generate'""", [project_id, list(effect_ids)])
+    result = {}
+    from .domain import canonical_hash
+    from .application import _command_from_payload
+    for effect_id, asset_id, digest, content, payload, source, source_hash in cursor.fetchall():
+        if digest != canonical_hash(content):
+            continue
+        contract = content['contract']
+        evidence = contract['evidence']
+        if not evidence.get('real_model_call') or evidence.get('fixture_protocol_only'):
+            continue
+        if source_hash != canonical_hash(source) or source_hash != contract['source']['content_hash']:
+            continue
+        try:
+            validate_world_generation_result(contract['output'], source['contract'], _command_from_payload(payload))
+        except KernelError as error:
+            if error.code not in WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS:
+                continue
+            result[effect_id] = {'accepted': False, 'failure_class':'SCHEMA_REJECTION',
+                'validation_kind':'STRUCTURAL', 'error_code':error.code,
+                'metric':'DOMAIN_GATE_ACCEPTANCE.v1', 'evidence_refs':['asset-version:'+asset_id]}
+    return result

+def test_frozen_template_configuration_definition_survives_v2_successor(monkeypatch):
+    from copy import deepcopy
+    import novel_forge.modules.authority_handler_runtime.policy_commands as module
+    harness = CapabilityPackPostgresHarness("creative-definition-successor")
+    operator = replace(harness.compiler, permissions=harness.compiler.permissions | {"book.authority.execute"})
+    value = template(harness.book_ids[0])
+    arguments = dict(database_url=DATABASE_URL, owner="PROVIDER_ROUTING", project_id=harness.project_id,
+        workspace_id=harness.workspace_id, principal=operator, application_build_id=harness.application_build_id,
+        commit_sha=harness.release_sha, trace_id=harness.project_id, correlation_id=harness.project_id)
+    current_definition = module.owned_policy_definition
+
+    def frozen_definition(**kwargs):
+        definition = deepcopy(current_definition(**kwargs))
+        definition.update(definition_version="1.6.0", command_schema_version="provider.routing_policy.configure.command.v6",
+            result_schema_version="provider.routing_policy.configure.result.v6")
+        for key in ("command_schema", "result_schema", "input_contract"):
+            schema = definition[key]["properties"]["policy"]["properties"]["execution_templates"]["items"]
+            schema["$defs"].pop("RepairRoutingConfiguration", None)
+            schema["properties"].pop("repair_routing", None)
+            schema["properties"]["version"].pop("enum", None)
+            schema["properties"]["version"]["const"] = "creative-execution-template.v1"
+        return definition
+
+    with monkeypatch.context() as legacy:
+        legacy.setattr(module, "owned_policy_definition", frozen_definition)
+        old = module.configure_owned_policy(**arguments, idempotency_key=harness.project_id+":v1",
+            content={"policy_version":"v1", "reason":"frozen historical contract", "task_overrides":[], "execution_templates":[value]})
+        old = deepcopy(old)
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(DATABASE_URL) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT definition_hash FROM authority_operation_definition WHERE name='provider.routing_policy.configure' AND definition_version='1.6.0'")
+        digest = uow.cursor.fetchone()[0]
+    value.update(version="creative-execution-template.v2", repair_routing={"version":"native-repair-routing.v1", "max_same_route_repairs":2})
+    module.configure_owned_policy(**arguments, idempotency_key=harness.project_id+":v2",
+        content={"policy_version":"v2", "reason":"explicit bounded repair successor", "task_overrides":[], "execution_templates":[value]})
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(DATABASE_URL) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT definition_hash FROM authority_operation_definition WHERE name='provider.routing_policy.configure' AND definition_version='1.6.0'")
+        assert uow.cursor.fetchone()[0] == digest
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT content FROM authority_runtime_policy_version WHERE project_id=%s AND owner='PROVIDER_ROUTING' AND policy_version='v1'", [harness.project_id])
+        assert uow.cursor.fetchone()[0] == old["policy"]
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM authority_external_effect WHERE project_id=%s", [harness.project_id])
+        assert uow.cursor.fetchone()[0] == 0

+def _recover_capacity_formation(*, harness, chapter_pack, principal, pack_actor, settings, env, registry, transport, admitted, monkeypatch, routing=False, input_order=False,world_optional=False,stage_repairs=False):
+    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.cancellation import CancelCommand, request_cancellation
+    from novel_forge.modules.capability_packs.validation_deployment import register_validation_runtime_definitions, install_chapter_validation_pack
+    from tests.integration.creative_validation_production_support import install_planning
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.runtime_continuation import require_binding
+    with AuthorityUnitOfWork(DATABASE_URL) as uow:
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT o.operation_id,o.version,o.command_payload->'payload' FROM authority_operation o JOIN authority_story_planning_generation g USING(operation_id) WHERE o.project_id=%s AND o.status=%s AND (NOT %s OR g.status='REJECTED' AND g.candidate_asset_version_id IS NOT NULL)", [harness.project_id,"FAILED_FINAL" if input_order or stage_repairs else "FAILED_RETRYABLE",stage_repairs])
+        source, version, original = uow.cursor.fetchone()
+        uow.cursor.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM authority_external_effect WHERE operation_id=%s", [source])
+        assert uow.cursor.fetchone()[0] == (3 if stage_repairs else 2 if world_optional else 0)
+    before = len(transport.calls)
+    if input_order or world_optional or stage_repairs:
+        cancelled = {"status":"FAILED_FINAL" if input_order else 'FAILED_RETRYABLE', "version":version}
+    else:
+        cancelled = request_cancellation(DATABASE_URL,CancelCommand(operation_id=source,project_id=harness.project_id,
+            actor_id=principal.principal_id,expected_version=version,reason="native capacity successor; zero dispatch",
+            idempotency_key=source+":capacity-cancel",trace_id=harness.project_id,correlation_id=harness.project_id),settle_undispatched=True)
+        assert cancelled["status"] == "CANCELLED"
+    sha = "f"*40
+    hashes = register_validation_runtime_definitions(DATABASE_URL,application_build_id=harness.application_build_id,commit_sha=sha)
+    harness.release_sha=sha
+    if routing or world_optional or stage_repairs:
+        from novel_forge.modules.authority_handler_runtime.policy_commands import configure_owned_policy
+        from tests.unit.test_creative_execution_template import template
+        value = template(harness.book_ids[0], version="creative-execution-template.v2", repair_routing={
+            "version": "native-repair-routing.v1", "max_same_route_repairs": 2})
+        value["allowed_candidates"] = ["codex-cli:gpt-test:high"]
+        if world_optional:
+            value.update(version='creative-execution-template.v3',world_future_preparation='DISABLED')
+        if stage_repairs:
+            value.update(version='creative-execution-template.v4',world_future_preparation='DISABLED',
+                repair_routing={'version':'native-repair-routing.v2','max_same_route_repairs':2})
+        configure_owned_policy(database_url=DATABASE_URL, owner="PROVIDER_ROUTING", project_id=harness.project_id,
+            workspace_id=harness.workspace_id, principal=pack_actor, application_build_id=harness.application_build_id,
+            commit_sha=sha, idempotency_key=harness.project_id+":repair-policy-v2", trace_id=harness.project_id,
+            correlation_id=harness.project_id, content={"policy_version":"creative-e2e.v2", "task_overrides":[],
+                "reason":"explicit successor for undispatched native repair", "execution_templates":[value]})
+    harness.pack_id += ".capacity"
+    chapter_pack.pack_id += ".capacity"
+    install_chapter_validation_pack(DATABASE_URL,repository_root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2],project_id=harness.project_id,
+        workspace_id=harness.workspace_id,book_id=harness.book_ids[0],application_build_id=harness.application_build_id,commit_sha=sha,
+        compiler=pack_actor,approver=pack_actor,agent_admin=pack_actor,pack_id_override=chapter_pack.pack_id)
+    profile=build_story_planning_pack_profile(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2],operation_definition_hash=hashes["planning"],
+        pack_id=harness.pack_id,planning_task_token_budget=None if routing else 128000)
+    install_planning(harness,profile,pack_actor)
+    env.update(NOVEL_FORGE_COMMIT_SHA=sha,NOVEL_FORGE_PLANNING_CAPABILITY_PACK_ID=harness.pack_id,NOVEL_FORGE_CHAPTER_CAPABILITY_PACK_ID=chapter_pack.pack_id)
+    execution=build_production_execution_composition(settings,env=env,provider_registry=registry,planning_fixture_factory=transport.planning,chapter_fixture_factory=transport.chapter)
+    chapter=build_chapter_authority_application(settings,execution=execution,env=env)
+    composition=build_book_production_composition(settings,principal=principal,execution=execution,chapter_application=chapter,env=env)
+    formation=composition.formation
+    planning=formation.generation.planning
+    if world_optional or stage_repairs:
+        return formation,execution,chapter,composition
+    def register():
+        return planning.register_runtime_continuation(source,project_id=harness.project_id,principal=principal,
+            expected_source_version=cancelled["version"],decision_evidence={"authorization_ref":"member-grant",
+                "authorized_reasoning_effort":"high","reason":"same task and repair count; capacity only"},trace_id=harness.project_id,correlation_id=harness.project_id)
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime import preflight_continuation
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+    with monkeypatch.context() as strict:
+        verified=preflight_continuation.verify_template_provenance
+        strict.setattr(preflight_continuation,"verify_template_provenance",lambda *args: {**verified(*args),"mode":"STRICT_PRODUCTION"})
+        with pytest.raises(KernelError,match="PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_FAILURE_NOT_ALLOWED" if routing and not input_order else "PLANNING_RUNTIME_CONTINUATION_POLICY_REQUIRED"):
+            register()
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
+        records=list(pool.map(lambda _:register(),range(2)))
+    assert len({r["operation_id"] for r in records})==1 and len(transport.calls)==before
+    binding=require_binding(DATABASE_URL,records[0]["continuation_id"])
+    assert binding["source_command"]==original
+    assert binding["successor_command"]["repair_number"]==original["repair_number"]==(2 if input_order else 1)
+    if not routing:
+        failed=formation.require(admitted.formation_run_id)
+        formation.resume(admitted.formation_run_id,expected_version=failed["version"],principal=principal)
+    return formation,execution,chapter,composition
+

+from copy import deepcopy
+
+import pytest
+
+from novel_forge.modules.book_authority import execution_recovery as recovery
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+
+
+class Cursor:
+    def __init__(self, root, effect, *, lease=False, accepted=False):
+        self.responses = iter([(root,), [effect], (lease,), (accepted,)])
+
+    def execute(self, *args):
+        pass
+
+    def fetchone(self):
+        return next(self.responses)
+
+    def fetchall(self):
+        return next(self.responses)
+
+
+class Principal:
+    principal_id = "operator"
+
+    def require(self, permission, project):
+        assert permission in {"book.authority.execute", "chapter.authority.execute"}
+        assert project == "project"
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def source(monkeypatch):
+    template = {
+        "mode": "CREATIVE_VALIDATION",
+        "book_id": "book",
+        "member_authorization_ref": "grant",
+        "require_literary_qualification": False,
+        "require_independent_review": True,
+    }
+    monkeypatch.setattr(
+        recovery,
+        "selected_book_template",
+        lambda *a, **k: {
+            "policy_snapshot": {"policy": {"execution_templates": [template]}}
+        },
+    )
+    monkeypatch.setattr(
+        recovery, "verify_template_provenance", lambda *a: deepcopy(template)
+    )
+    root = {
+        "operation_id": "root",
+        "definition_version": "3.8.0",
+        "command_payload": {},
+        "actor_id": "operator",
+        "status": "WAITING_CHILDREN",
+    }
+    book = {
+        "project_id": "project",
+        "book_id": "book",
+        "state": "CHAPTER_RUNNING",
+        "active_chapter_request_id": "request",
+        "accepted_through_chapter": 0,
+    }
+    request = {
+        "chapter_root_operation_id": "root",
+        "chapter_request_id": "request",
+        "chapter_number": 1,
+    }
+    candidate = {
+        "provider": "codex-cli",
+        "pricing": {"accounting_basis": "ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT"},
+        "authorization_ref": "grant",
+    }
+    payload = {
+        "_production_execution": {
+            "capability_runtime": {
+                "routing_decision": {"selected": {"candidate": candidate}}
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    error = {
+        "production_execution": {
+            "execution_result": {"outcome": "TIMED_OUT"},
+            "process_evidence": {"failure_code": "PROCESS_TIMEOUT"},
+        }
+    }
+    effect = [
+        "effect",
+        "FAILED_RETRYABLE",
+        "hash",
+        payload,
+        error,
+        "SETTLED",
+        "chapter.memo.generate",
+        "60",
+    ]
+    return root, book, request, effect, candidate, error

+@pytest.mark.parametrize(
+    "boundary",
+    [
+        "known",
+        "unknown",
+        "success",
+        "held-reserve",
+        "paid",
+        "output",
+        "foreign-actor",
+        "accepted-frontier",
+        "changed-grant",
+        "lease",
+        "accepted",
+        "strict",
+    ],
+)
+def test_only_known_preprose_member_timeout_is_eligible(source, monkeypatch, boundary):
+    root, book, request, effect, candidate, error = source
+    if boundary == "unknown":
+        effect[1] = "UNKNOWN"
+    if boundary == "success":
+        effect[1] = "SUCCEEDED"
+    if boundary == "held-reserve":
+        effect[5] = "HELD"
+    if boundary == "paid":
+        candidate["pricing"]["accounting_basis"] = "API_USAGE"
+    if boundary == "output":
+        error["production_execution"]["execution_result"]["output_manifest_ref"] = (
+            "partial"
+        )
+    if boundary == "foreign-actor":
+        root["actor_id"] = "foreign"
+    if boundary == "accepted-frontier":
+        book["accepted_through_chapter"] = 1
+    if boundary == "changed-grant":
+        candidate["authorization_ref"] = "other-grant"
+    if boundary == "strict":
+        monkeypatch.setattr(recovery, "selected_book_template", lambda *a, **k: None)
+    cursor = Cursor(
+        root, effect, lease=boundary == "lease", accepted=boundary == "accepted"
+    )
+    if boundary == "lease":
+        with pytest.raises(KernelError, match="Wait for the original execution lease"):
+            recovery.source_evidence(
+                cursor, book=book, request=request, principal=Principal()
+            )
+    else:
+        result = recovery.source_evidence(
+            cursor, book=book, request=request, principal=Principal()
+        )
+        assert bool(result) == (boundary == "known")
+
+

+def test_literal_null_branch_retains_nullable_const_and_rejects_other_types():
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.provider_output_contracts import _validate
+    schema = {"type":"object", "required":["value"], "additionalProperties":False,
+        "properties":{"value":{"anyOf":[{"type":"null"},{"type":"string"}]}}}
+    _validate(schema, {"value":None}, path="$")
+    _validate(schema, {"value":"text"}, path="$")
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        _validate(schema, {"value":1}, path="$")
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        _validate({"type":["string","null"],"const":"frozen"}, None, path="$")

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('stage', ['review','rereview'])
+def test_delivered_knowledge_schema_rejects_catalog_only_judgments(stage):
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.provider_output_contracts import bind_delivered_knowledge_scope
+    base = chapter_provider_output_schema(stage)
+    check = {'knowledge_card_asset_version_id':'catalog-only', 'status':'NOT_USED','reason':'inspiration',
+        'evidence':None, 'conditions_checked':[], 'exceptions_checked':[], 'issue_codes':[]}
+    value = {'score':80, 'issues':[], 'resolution_checks':[], 'knowledge_checks':[check]}
+    empty = bind_delivered_knowledge_scope(base, [])
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError,match='SCHEMA_INVALID'):
+        validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema(stage, empty, value)
+    validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema(stage, empty, {**value,'knowledge_checks':[]})
+    scoped = bind_delivered_knowledge_scope(base,[{'knowledge_card_asset_version_id':'delivered:v1'}])
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError,match='SCHEMA_INVALID'):
+        validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema(stage, scoped, value)
+    validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema(stage, scoped, {**value,'knowledge_checks':[{**check,'knowledge_card_asset_version_id':'delivered:v1'}]})
+    assert base['properties']['knowledge_checks'].get('maxItems') is None

+def test_shared_canon_schema_restores_every_inline_constraint_and_rejects_invalid_outputs():
+    import json
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.provider_output_contracts import shared_canon_schema
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.execution_gateway import PostgresChapterExecutionContractBinder
+    original, old_ref = PostgresChapterExecutionContractBinder._provider_output_contract(
+        stage='canon_extraction',request_payload={'input':{}},prompt_contract_version='v9')
+    shared, new_ref = PostgresChapterExecutionContractBinder._provider_output_contract(
+        stage='canon_extraction',request_payload={'input':{}},prompt_contract_version='v10')
+    def expand(value):
+        if isinstance(value,dict):
+            if '$ref' in value:
+                return expand(shared['$defs'][value['$ref'].split('/')[-1]])
+            return {key:expand(item) for key,item in value.items() if key!='$defs'}
+        if isinstance(value,list):
+            return [expand(item) for item in value]
+        return value
+    assert expand(shared) == original
+    assert old_ref.endswith(':v22') and new_ref.endswith(':v24')
+    assert len(json.dumps(shared)) < len(json.dumps(original))
+    candidate = {key:[] for key in original['properties'] if original['properties'][key].get('type')=='array'}
+    candidate.update(extraction_status='NO_CHANGE',no_change_reason='No observed change in this controlled schema probe.')
+    for schema in (original,shared):
+        validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,candidate)
+        broken = {**candidate,'invented_approval':True}
+        with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+            validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,broken)
+        with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+            validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,{**candidate,'canon_facts':[{}]})
+        broken = dict(candidate)
+        broken.pop('canon_facts')
+        with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+            validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,broken)
+    assert validate_chapter_provider_output_semantics('canon_extraction',candidate,output_schema_ref=old_ref) == validate_chapter_provider_output_semantics('canon_extraction',candidate,output_schema_ref=new_ref)
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError,match='CANON_SHARED_SCHEMA_SCOPE_INVALID'):
+        shared_canon_schema({'type':'object','$id':'nested-scope'})
+    malformed=deepcopy(shared)
+    name=next(iter(malformed['$defs']))
+    malformed['$defs'][name]={'$ref':'#/$defs/'+name}
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError,match='CHAPTER_PROVIDER_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_INVALID'):
+        validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',malformed,candidate)

+def test_codex_nullable_shared_object_preserves_closed_value_contract():
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.codex_configuration import codex_output_schema
+    from novel_forge.modules.chapter_authority.provider_output_contracts import validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema
+    from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+    original = {"$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema","type":"object","properties":{"value":{"$ref":"#/$defs/nullable"}},"required":["value"],"additionalProperties":False,
+        "$defs":{"nullable":{"type":["object","null"],"properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1}},"required":["id"],"additionalProperties":False}}}
+    projected = codex_output_schema(original)
+    assert projected['$defs']['nullable']['anyOf'][0]['type']=='object'
+    assert projected['$defs']['nullable']['anyOf'][1]=={'type':'null'}
+    for value in ({'value':None},{'value':{'id':'known'}}):
+        for schema in (original,projected):
+            validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,value)
+    for value in ({'value':{}},{'value':{'id':''}},{'value':{'id':'known','extra':True}},{'value':3}):
+        for schema in (original,projected):
+            with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+                validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('canon_extraction',schema,value)

+def test_codex_wire_correction_requires_exact_retained_hashes():
+    import hashlib
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.codex_configuration import codex_wire_correction_evidence
+    canonical={'type':'object','properties':{'value':{'type':['object','null'],'properties':{'id':{'type':'string'}},'required':['id'],'additionalProperties':False}},'required':['value'],'additionalProperties':False}
+    def digest(value):
+        return 'sha256:'+hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')).encode()).hexdigest()
+    evidence={'output_schema':canonical,'output_schema_version':'schema:chapter-provider-output:canon_extraction:v24',
+        'provider_schema_contract':{'version':'codex-structured-schema.v3','canonical_hash':digest(canonical),'provider_hash':digest(canonical)}}
+    proof=codex_wire_correction_evidence(evidence)
+    assert proof and proof['reversible_equivalence']=='EXACT'
+    assert proof['previous_wire_hash']!=proof['corrected_wire_hash']
+    evidence['provider_schema_contract']['provider_hash']='sha256:'+'0'*64
+    assert codex_wire_correction_evidence(evidence) is None
+    evidence['provider_schema_contract']['provider_hash']=digest(canonical)
+    evidence['output_schema_version']='schema:chapter-provider-output:canon_extraction:v23'
+    assert codex_wire_correction_evidence(evidence) is None
+

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.derived_step_revalidation import _settled_graph
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.chapter_quality_authority.context_views import _longform_chapter_plan_view
+
+
+class SettledEvidence:
+    def __init__(self, effects, *, recorded_failure=False, live_lease=False):
+        self.effects=effects
+        self.recorded_failure=recorded_failure
+        self.live_lease=live_lease
+        self.failure_lookup=False
+
+    def execute(self, query, params):
+        self.failure_lookup='known_failed_effects' in query
+
+    def fetchall(self):
+        return self.effects
+
+    def fetchone(self):
+        return (self.recorded_failure if self.failure_lookup else self.live_lease,)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('status,reservation,basis,authorization',[
+    ('UNKNOWN','HELD_UNKNOWN','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member'),
+    ('DISPATCHING','RESERVED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member'),
+    ('SUCCEEDED','RESERVED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member'),
+    ('SUCCEEDED','SETTLED','PRICED','paid'),
+    ('SUCCEEDED','SETTLED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT',''),
+    ('FAILED_FINAL','SETTLED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member'),
+])
+def test_revalidation_cannot_release_unknown_or_borrow_unrelated_failure(status,reservation,basis,authorization):
+    cursor=SettledEvidence([('effect','request-hash',status,reservation,basis,authorization)])
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+        _settled_graph(cursor,'root')
+
+
+def test_revalidation_retains_known_settled_calls_and_requires_no_live_lease():
+    records=[('producing','hash1','SUCCEEDED','SETTLED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member'),
+             ('schema-refusal','hash2','FAILED_FINAL','SETTLED','ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENT','member')]
+    assert _settled_graph(SettledEvidence(records,recorded_failure=True),'root')==[list(r) for r in records]
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError,match='DERIVED_STEP_LIVE_LEASE'):
+        _settled_graph(SettledEvidence(records,recorded_failure=True,live_lease=True),'root')
+
+
+def test_continuity_projection_keeps_declared_constraints_distinct_from_history():
+    plan={'chapter':1,'constraint_envelope':{'hard_constraints':['食火每日两次']},
+          'world_rule_usages':[{'rule':'食火','use':'一次启动'}],
+          'power_state_changes':[{'from':'','to':'NOVICE'}], 'scene_plan':[]}
+    legacy=_longform_chapter_plan_view(plan)
+    current=_longform_chapter_plan_view(plan,include_governed_constraints=True)
+    assert 'governed_plan_contract' not in legacy
+    assert current['governed_plan_contract']['constraint_envelope']==plan['constraint_envelope']
+    assert current['governed_plan_contract']['power_state_changes']==plan['power_state_changes']
+    assert 'neither accepted past events' in current['governed_plan_contract']['evidence_scope']

+def test_taxonomy_repair_requires_only_unknown_labels_and_fresh_independent_review():

+        changed=deepcopy(review)
+        changed['claims'][0][field]=value
+        assert support_kind_repair_diagnostic(extraction,changed) is None
+    for changed in [dict(review,coverage='PARTIAL'),dict(review,claims=[]),dict(review,claims=review['claims']*2)]:
+        assert support_kind_repair_diagnostic(extraction,changed) is None

+from copy import deepcopy
+import json
+
+import pytest
+
+from novel_forge.modules.command_kernel.errors import KernelError
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.cost_routing import CostAwareRouter, RoutingDecision
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template import template_decision, validate_templates
+from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing import REPAIR_VERSION, verify_native_repair
+from tests.unit.test_cost_aware_routing import NOW, task
+from tests.unit.test_creative_execution_template import membership, template
+
+
+def repair_snapshot(*, number=1, failures=("SCHEMA_REJECTION",), **changes):
+    value = template(version="creative-execution-template.v2", repair_routing={
+        "version": "native-repair-routing.v1", "max_same_route_repairs": 2})
+    evidence = {"version": REPAIR_VERSION, "operation_id": "operation", "repair_number": number,
+        "parent_candidate_id": "member", "lineage_operation_ids": ["parent", "operation"]}
+    current = task(policy_snapshot={"policy": {"execution_templates": [value]}},
+        history=tuple({"candidate_id": "member", "failure_class": failure, "status": "SUCCEEDED"}
+            for failure in failures), lineage_operation_ids=("parent", "operation"),
+        declared_requirements={"native_planning_repair": evidence}, **changes)
+    return CostAwareRouter().explain(current, [membership()], {}, now=NOW).to_dict()
+
+
+def decide(snapshot):
+    return template_decision(RoutingDecision(json.dumps(snapshot))).to_dict()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("number,failures", [(1, ("SCHEMA_REJECTION",)),
+    (2, ("SCHEMA_REJECTION", "MALFORMED_STRUCTURED_OUTPUT"))])
+def test_explicit_native_bounded_repair_retains_failures_and_requires_contract(number, failures):
+    result = decide(repair_snapshot(number=number, failures=failures))
+    assert result["status"] == "SELECTED"
+    admission = result["selected"]["native_repair_admission"]
+    assert len(admission["retained_failed_outcomes"]) == number
+    assert admission["candidate_contract_acceptance"] == "REQUIRED_AFTER_REPAIR"
+    assert admission["literary_qualification"] == "NOT_RUN"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", ["v1", "strict", "missing", "wrong-operation", "wrong-lineage", "wrong-route", "counter", "quality", "unknown", "too-many"])
+def test_unrelated_or_unproven_failure_cannot_receive_native_repair_route(change):
+    snapshot = repair_snapshot()
+    value = snapshot["task"]["policy_snapshot"]["policy"]["execution_templates"][0]
+    evidence = snapshot["task"]["declared_requirements"]["native_planning_repair"]
+    if change == "v1":
+        value["version"] = "creative-execution-template.v1"
+        value.pop("repair_routing")
+    elif change == "strict":
+        value.update(mode="STRICT_PRODUCTION", require_literary_qualification=True)
+    elif change == "missing":
+        snapshot["task"]["declared_requirements"].clear()
+    elif change == "wrong-operation":
+        evidence["operation_id"] = "other"
+    elif change == "wrong-lineage":
+        evidence["lineage_operation_ids"] = ["other", "operation"]
+    elif change == "wrong-route":
+        evidence["parent_candidate_id"] = "other"
+    elif change == "counter":
+        evidence["repair_number"] = 3
+    else:
+        failures = {"quality": ("QUALITY_FAILURE",), "unknown": ("UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL_OUTCOME",),
+            "too-many": ("SCHEMA_REJECTION", "SCHEMA_REJECTION")}[change]
+        snapshot = repair_snapshot(failures=failures)
+    assert decide(snapshot)["status"] == "BLOCKED"
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("reason", ["CONTEXT_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED", "PROVIDER_TASK_NOT_AUTHORIZED",
+    "CROSS_MODEL_REVIEW_DIFFERENT_PROVIDER_REQUIRED", "RECONCILIATION_OR_POLICY_RESOLUTION_REQUIRED",
+    "MAX_CHAIN_ATTEMPTS_REACHED", "RETRY_AFTER_NOT_ELAPSED"])
+def test_native_contract_repair_never_removes_other_guards(reason):
+    snapshot = repair_snapshot()
+    snapshot["candidates"][0]["rejected_reasons"].append(reason)
+    result = decide(snapshot)
+    assert result["status"] == "BLOCKED"
+    assert reason in result["candidates"][0]["rejected_reasons"]
+
+
+def test_native_repair_evidence_is_rechecked_at_dispatch(monkeypatch):
+    snapshot = repair_snapshot()
+    evidence = deepcopy(snapshot["task"]["declared_requirements"]["native_planning_repair"])
+    import novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing as module
+    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "native_planning_repair", lambda *args, **kwargs: evidence)
+    verify_native_repair(None, snapshot)
+    evidence["repair_number"] = 2
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError, match="EVIDENCE_DRIFT"):
+        verify_native_repair(None, snapshot)

+def test_native_input_scope_keeps_both_ordered_hashes_and_rejects_artifact_drift(monkeypatch):
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing import native_planning_repair, LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION
+    from novel_forge.modules.story_planning_runtime.domain import canonical_hash
+    import novel_forge.modules.production_execution.execution_template as module
+    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "verify_template_provenance", lambda *args: {
+        "mode":"CREATIVE_VALIDATION", "require_literary_qualification":False})
+    parent_inputs = [{"role":role,"asset_version_id":role+":version", "content_hash":"sha256:"+letter*64,
+        "asset_type":"plan"} for role,letter in [("book","a"),("volume","b")]]
+    current_inputs = list(reversed(deepcopy(parent_inputs)))
+    command = {"repair_of_candidate_id":"candidate", "repair_number":2, "logical_key":"plan"}
+    parent = ("parent","SUCCEEDED",1,"result-hash","effect",{"selected":{"candidate":{"candidate_id":"member"}}},
+        canonical_hash(parent_inputs),parent_inputs)
+
+    class Cursor:
+        def execute(self, *args):
+            pass
+
+        def fetchone(self):
+            return command,canonical_hash(current_inputs),current_inputs
+
+        def fetchall(self):
+            return [parent]
+
+    args = dict(project_id="project", book_id="book", operation_id="operation", task_type="chapter_group_plan.generate",
+        lineage=("root","parent","operation"))
+    evidence = native_planning_repair(Cursor(), **args)
+    assert evidence["input_snapshot_hash"] != evidence["parent_input_snapshot_hash"]
+    assert evidence["input_scope_hash"] == canonical_hash({item["role"]:item for item in parent_inputs})
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError, match="NATIVE_PLANNING_REPAIR_SOURCE_INVALID"):
+        native_planning_repair(Cursor(), **args, evidence_version=LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION)
+    current_inputs[0]["asset_version_id"] = "substituted"
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError, match="PLANNING_GENERATION_INPUT_SNAPSHOT_DRIFT"):
+        native_planning_repair(Cursor(), **args)
+    current_inputs[:] = deepcopy(parent_inputs)
+    legacy = native_planning_repair(Cursor(), **args, evidence_version=LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION)
+    assert legacy["version"] == LEGACY_REPAIR_VERSION
+    assert "parent_input_snapshot_hash" not in legacy

+class Cursor:
+    def __init__(self, rows):
+        self.rows = rows
+
+    def execute(self, _query, args):
+        assert args == [["root", "world1", "world2", "deferred", "main1", "resumed"]]
+
+    def fetchall(self):
+        return self.rows
+
+
+def rows():
+    world = {
+        "generator_operation_name": "chapter_plan.generate",
+        "repair_result_diagnostic": {
+            "error_code": sorted(WORLD_GENERATION_CONTRACT_ERRORS)[0]
+        },
+    }
+    main = {"generator_operation_name": "chapter_plan.generate"}
+    return [
+        ("root", 0, main, "w0", "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "world_candidates.generate"),
+        ("world1", 1, world, "w1", "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "world_candidates.generate"),
+        ("world2", 2, world, "w2", "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "world_candidates.generate"),
+        (
+            "deferred",
+            2,
+            world,
+            "m0",
+            "FAILED_FINAL",
+            "SETTLED",
+            "chapter_plan.generate",
+        ),
+        ("main1", 3, main, "m1", "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "chapter_plan.generate"),
+        ("resumed", 3, main, "m1r", "SUCCEEDED", "SETTLED", "chapter_plan.generate"),
+    ]

+def test_stage_policy_admits_monotonic_third_repair_as_first_chapter_repair_only():
+    from tests.unit.test_native_planning_repair_routing import repair_snapshot, decide
+    from novel_forge.modules.production_execution.planning_repair_routing import (
+        STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION,
+    )
+    from copy import deepcopy
+
+    snapshot = repair_snapshot()
+    template = snapshot["task"]["policy_snapshot"]["policy"]["execution_templates"][0]
+    template.update(
+        version="creative-execution-template.v4", world_future_preparation="DISABLED"
+    )
+    template["repair_routing"]["version"] = "native-repair-routing.v2"
+    evidence = snapshot["task"]["declared_requirements"]["native_planning_repair"]
+    evidence.update(
+        version=STAGE_REPAIR_VERSION,
+        repair_number=3,
+        stage_repair_number=1,
+        repair_stage=snapshot["task"]["task_type"],
+    )
+    assert decide(snapshot)["status"] == "SELECTED"
+    for change in (
+        "exhausted",
+        "wrong-stage",
+        "legacy-evidence",
+        "independent",
+        "unknown",
+    ):
+        invalid = deepcopy(snapshot)
+        proof = invalid["task"]["declared_requirements"]["native_planning_repair"]
+        if change == "exhausted":
+            proof["stage_repair_number"] = 3
+        elif change == "wrong-stage":
+            proof["repair_stage"] = "world_candidates.generate"
+        elif change == "legacy-evidence":
+            proof["version"] = "native-planning-repair-evidence.v2"
+        elif change == "independent":
+            invalid["candidates"][0]["rejected_reasons"].append(
+                "CROSS_MODEL_REVIEW_DIFFERENT_PROVIDER_REQUIRED"
+            )
+        else:
+            invalid["task"]["history"][0]["failure_class"] = "UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL_OUTCOME"
+        assert decide(invalid)["status"] == "BLOCKED", change

+        item['evidence_locations']=[{'quote':'她关上门。','claim':'The narrated action is legible.'}]
+        if item['dimension']=='golden_opening':
+            item.update(gate_status='NOT_APPLICABLE',score=None,evidence_locations=[])
+    validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('quality_commercial',bound,result)
+    invalid=deepcopy(result)
+    next(i for i in invalid['dimensions'] if i['dimension']=='toxic_point').update(gate_status='NOT_APPLICABLE',score=None,evidence_locations=[])
+    with pytest.raises(KernelError):
+        validate_chapter_provider_output_against_schema('quality_commercial',bound,invalid)
